===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/controls/binder.ts =====
// Reasonableness-review binder (RC-01): one project's evidence assembled live from the store,
// rendered to standalone HTML (numbered sections) and a flat CSV extract. Used by Evidence and Regulatory.
import type { AppState } from '@/core/types'
import { categoryName, personName } from '@/core/store'
import { position, snapshotsFor } from '@/core/engine/position'
import { estimateTotals } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { fmtDate, fmtDateTime, money, sum } from '@/core/util'
import { approvalChain, assessEvidence, esc, fingerprint, SNAP_META, snapshotAuthority, stageLabel } from './trace'

const td = (x: unknown, num = false) => `<td${num ? ' class="num"' : ''}>${esc(x)}</td>`
const th = (...h: string[]) => `<tr>${h.map((x) => `<th>${esc(x)}</th>`).join('')}</tr>`
const table = (head: string[], rows: string[][], numCols: number[] = []) =>
  rows.length ? `<table>${th(...head)}${rows.map((r) => `<tr>${r.map((c, i) => td(c, numCols.includes(i))).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</table>` : '<p class="meta">None on record.</p>'

export function binderData(s: AppState, pid: string) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)!
  const prog = s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)!
  const pos = position(s, pid)
  const ests = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version)
  const snaps = snapshotsFor(s, pid)
  const changes = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code))
  const variances = s.variances.filter((v) => v.projectId === pid)
  const risks = s.risks.filter((r) => r.projectId === pid)
  const draws = s.contingencyDraws.filter((d) => d.projectId === pid)
  const gates = s.gateDecisions.filter((g) => g.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date))
  const recs = s.records.filter((r) => r.projectId === pid)
  const plans = s.safetyPlans.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid)
  const events = s.safetyEvents.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid)
  const tailboards = s.tailboards.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid)
  const assess = assessEvidence(s, pid)
  return { p, prog, pos, ests, snaps, changes, variances, risks, draws, gates, recs, plans, events, tailboards, assess }
}

/** HTML body for one project's binder. `n` prefixes section numbers (program binders nest projects). */
export function projectBinderBody(s: AppState, pid: string, prefix = ''): string {
  const d = binderData(s, pid)
  const { p, prog, pos } = d
  const N = (i: number) => `${prefix}${i}`
  const out: string[] = []
  const realized = d.risks.filter((r) => r.status === 'realized')
  const drawn = pos.contingencyUsed

  out.push(`<h2>${N(1)}. Cover &amp; financial position</h2>`)
  out.push(table(['Item', 'Value'], [
    ['Project', `${p.code} ${p.name}`], ['Program', `${prog.code} — ${prog.name}`], ['Regulatory driver', p.regulatoryCitation],
    ['Proceeding', p.fundingProceeding], ['Recovery mechanism', prog.recovery], ['Stage / status', `${stageLabel(s, p, p.stageIndex)} · ${p.status}`],
    ['Original authorized budget', money(pos.originalBudget, 'full')], ['Current authorized budget', money(pos.currentBudget, 'full')],
    ['Recorded cost to date (ERP actuals)', money(pos.actualToDate, 'full')], ['Open commitments', money(pos.commitments, 'full')],
    ['Forecast at completion (EAC)', money(pos.eac, 'full')], ['Variance to current budget', money(pos.variance, 'full')],
    ['Contingency original / drawn / remaining', `${money(pos.contingencyOriginal, 'full')} / ${money(drawn, 'full')} / ${money(pos.contingencyRemaining, 'full')}`],
    ['Evidence completeness', `${d.assess.score}% (${d.assess.passed} of ${d.assess.checks.length} checks pass)`],
  ]))

  out.push(`<h2>${N(2)}. Estimate history</h2>`)
  out.push(table(['Ver', 'Class', 'Title', 'Status', 'Total', 'Contingency', 'Approved by', 'Approved'], d.ests.map((e) => { const t = estimateTotals(e); return [`v${e.version}`, `Class ${e.cls}`, e.title, e.status, money(t.total, 'full'), `${e.contingency.pct}% (${money(t.contingency)})`, personName(s, e.approvedById), fmtDateTime(e.approvedAt)] }), [4]))
  for (const e of d.ests.filter((e) => e.status !== 'draft')) {
    out.push(`<h3>${N(2)}.${e.version} Basis of estimate — v${e.version}</h3><p><b>Scope basis.</b> ${esc(e.basis.scopeBasis)}</p><p><b>Class justification.</b> ${esc(e.basis.classJustification)}</p><p><b>Contingency rationale.</b> ${esc(e.basis.contingencyRationale)}</p><p><b>Assumptions.</b> ${esc(e.basis.assumptions.join('; ') || 'None recorded')}</p><p><b>Exclusions.</b> ${esc(e.basis.exclusions.join('; ') || 'None recorded')}</p>`)
    const chain = approvalChain(s, e.workflowInstanceId)
    if (chain.length) out.push(table(['When', 'Who', 'Step', 'Action', 'E-signature'], chain.map((c) => [fmtDateTime(c.at), c.actor, c.step, c.action, c.sig ?? ''])))
  }

  out.push(`<h2>${N(3)}. Baselines and their approving decisions</h2>`)
  const bl = d.snaps.filter((x) => x.type !== 'period-close')
  out.push(table(['Date', 'Type', 'Label', 'Budget', 'EAC', 'Contingency left', 'Authority'], bl.map((x) => { const a = snapshotAuthority(s, x); return [fmtDate(x.date), SNAP_META[x.type].label + (x.proceeding ? ` (${x.proceeding})` : ''), x.label, money(x.budget.total, 'full'), money(x.budget.eac, 'full'), money(x.budget.contingencyRemaining, 'full'), `${a.kind}: ${a.text}${a.signers.length ? ' — ' + a.signers.map((g) => g.name + (g.sig ? ` [sig ${g.sig}]` : '')).join('; ') : ''}`] }), [3, 4, 5]))
  out.push(`<p class="meta">${d.snaps.filter((x) => x.type === 'period-close').length} monthly period-close snapshots are also on file.</p>`)

  out.push(`<h2>${N(4)}. Changes and approval chains</h2>`)
  out.push(table(['Code', 'Title', 'Root cause', 'Category', 'Cost', 'Days', 'Funded from', 'Status', 'Decided'], d.changes.map((c) => [c.code, c.title, c.rootCause, categoryName(s, c.categoryId), money(c.costImpact, 'full'), String(c.scheduleImpactDays), c.fundedFrom, c.status, fmtDate(c.decidedAt)]), [4, 5]))
  for (const c of d.changes) {
    const chain = approvalChain(s, c.workflowInstanceId)
    const res = s.snapshots.find((x) => x.id === c.resultingSnapshotId)
    out.push(`<h3>${esc(c.code)} — ${esc(c.title)}</h3><p>${esc(c.description)}${c.scopeImpact && c.scopeImpact !== 'None' ? ` Scope impact: ${esc(c.scopeImpact)}.` : ''}${res ? ` Resulting baseline: <b>${esc(res.label)}</b> (${fmtDate(res.date)}, ${money(res.budget.total, 'full')}).` : ''}${c.riskId ? ` Linked risk: ${esc(s.risks.find((r) => r.id === c.riskId)?.code)}.` : ''}</p>`)
    out.push(table(['When', 'Who', 'Step', 'Action', 'Comment', 'E-signature'], chain.map((x) => [fmtDateTime(x.at), `${x.actor}${x.title ? ', ' + x.title : ''}`, x.step, x.action, x.comment ?? '', x.sig ?? ''])))
  }

  out.push(`<h2>${N(5)}. Variance explanations</h2>`)
  out.push(table(['Date', 'Metric', 'What crossed the threshold', 'Amount', 'Root cause', 'Narrative', 'Linked changes', 'Status'], d.variances.map((v) => [fmtDate(v.date), v.metric.toUpperCase(), v.description, v.metric === 'eac' ? money(v.amount, 'full') : `${v.amount} days`, v.rootCause ?? '', v.narrative || '(not written)', v.changeIds.map((id) => s.changes.find((c) => c.id === id)?.code).join(', '), v.status])))

  out.push(`<h2>${N(6)}. Risks realized vs contingency drawn</h2>`)
  out.push(`<p>Contingency at authorization ${money(pos.contingencyOriginal, 'full')}; drawn ${money(drawn, 'full')} through ${d.changes.filter((c) => c.status === 'approved' && c.fundedFrom === 'contingency').length} approved changes and ${d.draws.filter((x) => x.status === 'approved' && !x.changeId).length} direct draws; remaining ${money(pos.contingencyRemaining, 'full')}. Open risk exposure ${money(pos.riskExposure, 'full')}.</p>`)
  out.push(table(['Risk', 'Title', 'Status', 'Most-likely cost', 'Drawn against it'], realized.map((r) => [r.code, r.title, r.status, money(r.cost.ml, 'full'), money(sum(d.draws.filter((x) => x.riskId === r.id && x.status === 'approved').map((x) => x.amount)) + sum(d.changes.filter((c) => c.riskId === r.id && c.status === 'approved').map((c) => c.costImpact)), 'full')]), [3, 4]))
  out.push(table(['Draw date', 'Amount', 'Reason', 'Risk', 'Status'], d.draws.map((x) => [fmtDate(x.date), money(x.amount, 'full'), x.reason, s.risks.find((r) => r.id === x.riskId)?.code ?? '', x.status]), [1]))

  out.push(`<h2>${N(7)}. Safety record summary</h2>`)
  out.push(table(['Measure', 'Value'], [
    ['Job-specific safety plans', d.plans.map((x) => `${x.workPackage} v${x.version} (${x.status}${x.approvedAt ? ', approved ' + fmtDate(x.approvedAt) : ''})`).join('; ') || 'None'],
    ['Tailboards recorded', String(d.tailboards.length)],
    ['Observations / near misses / incidents', `${d.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'observation').length} / ${d.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'near-miss').length} / ${d.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'incident').length}`],
    ['SIF-potential events', `${d.events.filter((e) => e.sifPotential).length} (${d.events.filter((e) => e.sifPotential && e.status === 'open').length} open)`],
  ]))

  out.push(`<h2>${N(8)}. Records completeness</h2>`)
  const rc = { verified: d.recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'verified').length, attached: d.recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'attached').length, missing: d.recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'missing').length }
  out.push(d.recs.length ? `<p>${d.recs.length} records: ${rc.verified} verified, ${rc.attached} attached awaiting verification, ${rc.missing} missing.</p>` + table(['Type', 'Segment', 'Status', 'File', 'Verified by'], d.recs.filter((r) => r.status !== 'verified').map((r) => [r.type, r.segment, r.status, r.fileName ?? '', personName(s, r.verifiedById)])) : '<p class="meta">No construction records required at this stage.</p>')

  out.push(`<h2>${N(9)}. Gate decisions</h2>`)
  out.push(table(['Date', 'Gate', 'Outcome', 'Readiness', 'Decided by', 'Estimate', 'Notes'], d.gates.map((g) => [fmtDate(g.date), g.gateName, g.outcome, `${g.readiness}%`, g.decidedBy.map((id) => personName(s, id)).join(', '), (() => { const e = s.estimates.find((x) => x.id === g.estimateId); return e ? `v${e.version} Class ${e.cls}` : '' })(), g.notes])))

  out.push(`<h2>${N(10)}. Completeness assessment</h2>`)
  out.push(`<p><b>${d.assess.score}%</b> — ${d.assess.passed} of ${d.assess.checks.length} checks pass. Major checks weigh three times minor checks.</p>`)
  out.push(table(['Area', 'Severity', 'Gap'], d.assess.gaps.map((g) => [g.area, g.severity, g.gap ?? g.label])))
  return out.join('\n')
}

export function projectBinderHtml(s: AppState, pid: string): string {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)!
  const body = projectBinderBody(s, pid)
  const me = s.people.find((x) => x.id === s.currentUserId)
  const fp = fingerprint(body)
  return htmlWrap(`Reasonableness review package — ${p.code}`, `<h1>Reasonableness review package</h1><div class="meta">${esc(s.settings.orgName)} · ${esc(p.code)} ${esc(p.name)} · as of ${fmtDate(s.settings.asOf)} · assembled by ${esc(me?.name)} (${esc(me?.title)}) · content fingerprint ${fp}</div><p class="meta">Every figure in this package is generated from the system of record. Section 4 reproduces each change's workflow history, including e-signature references, unaltered.</p>${body}`)
}

function htmlWrap(title: string, body: string) {
  return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${esc(title)}</title><style>
  body{font-family:'Public Sans',system-ui,sans-serif;color:#141b24;max-width:980px;margin:40px auto;padding:0 24px;font-size:12.5px;line-height:1.5}
  h1{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 4px}h2{font-size:16px;border-bottom:2px solid #141b24;padding-bottom:4px;margin-top:30px;page-break-after:avoid}h3{font-size:13.5px;margin:18px 0 4px}
  table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:8px 0}th,td{border:1px solid #d6dce3;padding:4px 7px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}th{background:#e9ecf0;font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em}
  td.num{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;white-space:nowrap}.meta{color:#4f5a69}.toc a{color:#14457a}.proj{page-break-before:always}
  @media print{body{margin:0}}</style></head><body>${body}</body></html>`
}

export function programBinderHtml(s: AppState, programId: string, pids: string[]): string {
  const prog = s.programs.find((x) => x.id === programId)!
  const me = s.people.find((x) => x.id === s.currentUserId)
  const rows = pids.map((pid) => { const d = binderData(s, pid); return { pid, d } })
  const toc = rows.map(({ pid, d }, i) => `<tr><td>${i + 1}</td><td><a href="#${esc(pid)}">${esc(d.p.code)}</a></td><td>${esc(d.p.name)}</td><td class="num">${money(d.pos.currentBudget)}</td><td class="num">${money(d.pos.actualToDate)}</td><td class="num">${money(d.pos.eac)}</td><td class="num">${d.assess.score}%</td><td class="num">${d.assess.gaps.length}</td></tr>`).join('')
  const bodies = rows.map(({ pid, d }, i) => `<section class="proj" id="${esc(pid)}"><h1>${i + 1}. ${esc(d.p.code)} ${esc(d.p.name)}</h1>${projectBinderBody(s, pid, `${i + 1}.`)}</section>`).join('\n')
  const fp = fingerprint(bodies)
  return htmlWrap(`Program evidence package — ${prog.code}`, `<h1>Program evidence package — ${esc(prog.code)} ${esc(prog.name)}</h1><div class="meta">${esc(s.settings.orgName)} · ${esc(prog.regulatoryDriver)} · recovery: ${esc(prog.recovery)} · as of ${fmtDate(s.settings.asOf)} · assembled by ${esc(me?.name)} · fingerprint ${fp}</div><h2>Contents</h2><table class="toc"><tr><th>#</th><th>Project</th><th>Name</th><th>Budget</th><th>Recorded</th><th>EAC</th><th>Complete</th><th>Gaps</th></tr>${toc}</table>${bodies}`)
}

/** Flat CSV ledger: one row per evidence record. */
export function evidenceRows(s: AppState, pid: string): Record<string, unknown>[] {
  const d = binderData(s, pid)
  const code = d.p.code
  const rows: Record<string, unknown>[] = []
  const r = (section: string, record: string, date: string | undefined, description: string, amount: number | '', status: string, approver = '', signature = '') => rows.push({ project: code, program: d.prog.code, section, record, date: date ?? '', description, amount, status, approver, signature })
  r('Position', 'Current budget', d.p.dates.initiated, 'Current authorized budget incl. contingency', d.pos.currentBudget, d.p.status)
  r('Position', 'Recorded cost', undefined, 'ERP actuals to date', d.pos.actualToDate, '')
  r('Position', 'EAC', undefined, 'Forecast at completion', d.pos.eac, '')
  for (const e of d.ests) r('Estimate', `v${e.version}`, e.approvedAt?.slice(0, 10), `${e.title} (Class ${e.cls})`, estimateTotals(e).total, e.status, personName(s, e.approvedById))
  for (const x of d.snaps) { const a = snapshotAuthority(s, x); r('Baseline', SNAP_META[x.type].label, x.date, x.label, x.budget.total, a.kind, a.signers.map((g) => g.name).join('; '), a.signers.map((g) => g.sig).filter(Boolean).join(' ')) }
  for (const c of d.changes) {
    const chain = approvalChain(s, c.workflowInstanceId).filter((x) => x.action === 'approve')
    r('Change', c.code, c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt, `${c.title} [${c.rootCause}; ${c.fundedFrom}; ${c.scheduleImpactDays} days]`, c.costImpact, c.status, chain.map((x) => `${x.actor} (${x.step})`).join(' > '), chain.map((x) => x.sig).filter(Boolean).join(' '))
  }
  for (const v of d.variances) r('Variance', v.metric, v.date, `${v.description} — ${v.narrative}`, v.metric === 'eac' ? v.amount : '', v.status, personName(s, v.authorId))
  for (const x of d.draws) r('Contingency draw', s.risks.find((k) => k.id === x.riskId)?.code ?? '', x.date, x.reason, x.amount, x.status)
  for (const g of d.gates) r('Gate', g.gateName, g.date, g.notes, '', g.outcome, g.decidedBy.map((id) => personName(s, id)).join('; '))
  for (const k of d.recs) r('Record', k.type, undefined, k.segment, '', k.status, personName(s, k.verifiedById))
  for (const g of d.assess.gaps) r('Gap', g.area, s.settings.asOf, g.gap ?? g.label, '', g.severity)
  return rows
}

===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/controls/trace.ts =====
// Traceability logic shared by Baselines, Cost, Changes, Evidence and Regulatory (builder C).
// Pure functions over AppState: every figure here is derived from store records.
import type { AppState, Change, Project, Snapshot, SnapshotType, WorkflowInstance } from '@/core/types'
import { personName } from '@/core/store'
import { activeBudget, position, snapshotsFor, thresholdBreaches } from '@/core/engine/position'
import { evmMetrics } from '@/core/engine/evm'
import { templateFor } from '@/core/engine/gates'
import { daysBetween, fmtDate, money, sum } from '@/core/util'

// ───────────────────────────── Snapshot type presentation
export const SNAP_META: Record<SnapshotType, { label: string; color: string; shape: 'diamond' | 'circle' | 'tick' | 'square' | 'triangle' | 'ring'; tone: 'ink' | 'info' | 'neutral' | 'marker' | 'good' }> = {
  original: { label: 'Original', color: 'rgb(var(--ink))', shape: 'diamond', tone: 'ink' },
  current: { label: 'Current', color: 'rgb(var(--link))', shape: 'circle', tone: 'info' },
  'period-close': { label: 'Period close', color: 'rgb(var(--faint))', shape: 'tick', tone: 'neutral' },
  gate: { label: 'Gate', color: 'rgb(var(--marker))', shape: 'square', tone: 'marker' },
  regulatory: { label: 'Regulatory', color: 'rgb(var(--good))', shape: 'triangle', tone: 'good' },
  forecast: { label: 'Forecast', color: 'rgb(var(--info))', shape: 'ring', tone: 'info' },
}

/** Cross-tab focus: a link elsewhere can ask the Baselines tab to open a snapshot (hash routes carry no ids). */
let focusSnap: string | null = null
export const setFocusSnapshot = (id: string | null) => { focusSnap = id }
export const takeFocusSnapshot = () => { const x = focusSnap; focusSnap = null; return x }
let focusChange: string | null = null
export const setFocusChange = (id: string | null) => { focusChange = id }
export const takeFocusChange = () => { const x = focusChange; focusChange = null; return x }

// ───────────────────────────── Workflow approval chain
export interface ChainStep { at: string; actorId: string; actor: string; title: string; step: string; action: string; comment?: string; sig?: string }

export function approvalChain(s: AppState, instId?: string): ChainStep[] {
  const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === instId)
  if (!inst) return []
  const def = s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === inst.defId)
  const label = (id: string) => def?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === id)?.label ?? ''
  return inst.history
    .filter((h) => ['start', 'submit', 'approve', 'return', 'reject', 'recall', 'complete'].includes(h.action))
    .map((h) => ({ at: h.at, actorId: h.actorId, actor: personName(s, h.actorId), title: s.people.find((p) => p.id === h.actorId)?.title ?? '', step: h.action === 'complete' ? 'Workflow complete' : label(h.nodeId), action: h.action === 'complete' ? `complete — ${h.comment}` : h.action, comment: h.action === 'complete' ? undefined : h.comment, sig: h.signature?.hash }))
}

/** The final dollar-authority (or last) approval event of an instance. */
export function finalApprover(s: AppState, inst?: WorkflowInstance) {
  if (!inst) return undefined
  const ev = [...inst.history].reverse().find((h) => h.action === 'approve')
  return ev ? { name: personName(s, ev.actorId), title: s.people.find((p) => p.id === ev.actorId)?.title ?? '', at: ev.at, sig: ev.signature?.hash } : undefined
}

export function doaLabel(s: AppState, projectId: string, amount: number) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)
  const prog = s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p?.programId)
  const m = s.doaMatrices.find((x) => x.id === prog?.doaMatrixId) ?? s.doaMatrices[0]
  if (!m) return { label: '—', roleId: undefined as string | undefined, people: [] as string[], matrix: '' }
  const rows = m.rows.slice().sort((a, b) => a.upTo - b.upTo)
  const row = rows.find((r) => Math.abs(amount) <= r.upTo) ?? rows[rows.length - 1]
  const people = s.people.filter((x) => x.roles.includes(row.roleId) && !x.company).map((x) => x.name)
  return { label: row.label, roleId: row.roleId, people, matrix: `${m.name} v${m.version}` }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Snapshots & baselines
/** What authorized a snapshot: the gate decision, the approved change, or the person who froze it. */
export function snapshotAuthority(s: AppState, snap: Snapshot): { kind: string; text: string; signers: { name: string; at?: string; sig?: string }[]; changeId?: string; gateDecisionId?: string } {
  if (snap.changeId) {
    const c = s.changes.find((x) => x.id === snap.changeId)
    const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === c?.workflowInstanceId)
    const fa = finalApprover(s, inst)
    return { kind: 'Approved change', text: c ? `${c.code} ${c.title} — approved ${fmtDate(c.decidedAt)}${fa ? ` by ${fa.name}` : ''}` : 'Change record not found', signers: fa ? [{ name: `${fa.name}, ${fa.title}`, at: fa.at, sig: fa.sig }] : [], changeId: c?.id }
  }
  const gd = s.gateDecisions.find((g) => g.snapshotId === snap.id) ?? (snap.type === 'original' || snap.type === 'current' ? s.gateDecisions.find((g) => g.projectId === snap.projectId && g.date === snap.date && (g.outcome === 'go' || g.outcome === 'go-with-conditions')) : undefined)
  if (gd) return { kind: 'Gate decision', text: `${gd.gateName} — ${gd.outcome.replace(/-/g, ' ')} on ${fmtDate(gd.date)} (readiness ${gd.readiness}%)`, signers: gd.decidedBy.map((id) => ({ name: `${personName(s, id)}, ${s.people.find((p) => p.id === id)?.title ?? ''}` })), gateDecisionId: gd.id }
  const by = personName(s, snap.createdById)
  if (snap.type === 'regulatory') return { kind: 'Regulatory snapshot', text: `Frozen for ${snap.proceeding ?? 'regulatory filing'} by ${by}`, signers: [{ name: by }] }
  if (snap.type === 'period-close') return { kind: 'Monthly close', text: `Period close frozen by ${by} (project controls)`, signers: [{ name: by }] }
  if (snap.type === 'forecast') return { kind: 'Forecast snapshot', text: `Forecast position frozen by ${by}`, signers: [{ name: by }] }
  return { kind: 'Manual', text: `Taken by ${by}`, signers: [{ name: by }] }
}

/** Baseline (Original/Current) in force on a date, optionally ignoring one snapshot. */
export function baselineInForce(s: AppState, pid: string, date: string, excludeId?: string): Snapshot | undefined {
  return snapshotsFor(s, pid).filter((x) => (x.type === 'current' || x.type === 'original') && x.date <= date && x.id !== excludeId).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || (a.type === 'current' ? 1 : -1)).pop()
}

export const changeBaseline = (s: AppState, c: Change) => (c.baselineSnapshotId ? s.snapshots.find((x) => x.id === c.baselineSnapshotId) : baselineInForce(s, c.projectId, c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt, c.resultingSnapshotId))

/** Approved changes decided after snapshot A and up to snapshot B. */
export function changesBetween(s: AppState, pid: string, a: Snapshot, b: Snapshot): Change[] {
  const [lo, hi] = a.date <= b.date ? [a, b] : [b, a]
  return s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid && c.status === 'approved' && c.decidedAt && c.decidedAt >= lo.date && c.decidedAt <= hi.date && c.resultingSnapshotId !== lo.id)
}

export const stageLabel = (s: AppState, p: Project, idx: number) => templateFor(s, p).stages[idx]?.name ?? `Stage ${idx + 1}`

// ───────────────────────────── Forecast by year
const thisYear = (s: AppState) => Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))

export function actualsByYear(s: AppState, pid: string): Record<string, number> {
  const out: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const c of s.costPeriods.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid)) out[c.period.slice(0, 4)] = (out[c.period.slice(0, 4)] || 0) + c.actual
  return out
}

/** Forecast by year = recorded actuals for past years + (EAC − actual to date) spread by the latest forecast's profile. */
export function forecastByYear(s: AppState, pid: string): Record<string, number> {
  const pos = position(s, pid)
  const act = actualsByYear(s, pid)
  const y0 = thisYear(s)
  const out: Record<string, number> = {}
  for (const [y, v] of Object.entries(act)) out[y] = v
  const remaining = Math.max(0, pos.eac - pos.actualToDate)
  if (!pos.eac) {
    const b = activeBudget(s, pid)
    for (const [y, v] of Object.entries(b?.byYear ?? {})) if (Number(y) >= y0) out[y] = (out[y] || 0) + v
    return out
  }
  const fc = s.forecasts.filter((f) => f.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date)).pop()
  let prof = Object.entries(fc?.byYear ?? {}).filter(([y]) => Number(y) >= y0)
  if (!prof.length) prof = Object.entries(activeBudget(s, pid)?.byYear ?? {}).filter(([y]) => Number(y) >= y0)
  if (!prof.length) prof = [[String(y0), 1]]
  const W = sum(prof.map(([, v]) => v)) || 1
  let acc = 0
  prof.forEach(([y, v], i) => {
    const share = i === prof.length - 1 ? remaining - acc : Math.round((remaining * v) / W)
    acc += share
    out[y] = (out[y] || 0) + share
  })
  return out
}

export interface YearRow { year: number; authorized: number; planned: number; recorded: number; forecast: number; proceeding: string }

/** BL-08: authorized vs planned vs recorded vs forecast for a program (or all programs when programId is empty). */
export function programYears(s: AppState, programId?: string): YearRow[] {
  const progs = s.programs.filter((p) => !programId || p.id === programId)
  const projects = s.projects.filter((p) => progs.some((g) => g.id === p.programId) && p.status !== 'cancelled')
  const years = new Map<number, YearRow>()
  const row = (y: number) => { if (!years.has(y)) years.set(y, { year: y, authorized: 0, planned: 0, recorded: 0, forecast: 0, proceeding: '' }); return years.get(y)! }
  for (const f of s.funding.filter((f) => progs.some((g) => g.id === f.programId))) { const r = row(f.year); r.authorized += f.authorized; if (!r.proceeding.includes(f.proceeding)) r.proceeding = r.proceeding ? r.proceeding : f.proceeding }
  for (const p of projects) {
    for (const [y, v] of Object.entries(activeBudget(s, p.id)?.byYear ?? {})) row(Number(y)).planned += v
    for (const [y, v] of Object.entries(actualsByYear(s, p.id))) row(Number(y)).recorded += v
    for (const [y, v] of Object.entries(forecastByYear(s, p.id))) row(Number(y)).forecast += v
  }
  return [...years.values()].sort((a, b) => a.year - b.year)
}

// ───────────────────────────── ERP reconciliation (CC-01)
function h32(str: string) { let h = 0x811c9dc5; for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) { h ^= str.charCodeAt(i); h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) } return h >>> 0 }
/** Content fingerprint for signed exports (FNV-1a, two passes). Not a security control — a tamper-evidence aid. */
export const fingerprint = (str: string) => h32(str).toString(16).padStart(8, '0') + h32(str.split('').reverse().join('')).toString(16).padStart(8, '0')

export interface ReconRow { categoryId: string; name: string; element: string; budget: number; actual: number }
export interface Recon { rows: ReconRow[]; unmapped: { amount: number; period: string; element: string; text: string } | null; erpTotal: number; mainlineTotal: number; budgetTotal: number }

/**
 * Actuals by cost element vs budget by category. Cost periods carry project totals only, so ERP postings
 * by element are simulated deterministically from budget shares; the small unmapped line is drawn from the
 * latest period and labeled as simulated. ERP total always ties to Crosswalk actual to date.
 */
export function erpRecon(s: AppState, pid: string): Recon {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)!
  const b = activeBudget(s, pid)
  const pos = position(s, pid)
  const byCat: Record<string, number> = { ...(b?.byCategory ?? {}) }
  for (const c of s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid && c.status === 'approved' && (!b || (c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt) >= b.createdAt))) byCat[c.categoryId] = (byCat[c.categoryId] || 0) + c.costImpact
  const periods = s.costPeriods.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid).sort((a, c) => a.period.localeCompare(c.period))
  const last = periods[periods.length - 1]
  const mapped = s.audit.some((a) => a.entity === 'erp-recon' && a.entityId === `${pid}:${last?.period}`)
  const unmappedAmt = last && !mapped ? Math.round(last.actual * 0.0062) : 0
  const toSpread = pos.actualToDate - unmappedAmt
  const cats = Object.keys(byCat).filter((k) => byCat[k] > 0)
  const w = cats.map((k) => byCat[k] * (0.82 + (h32(pid + k) % 1000) / 2800))
  const W = sum(w) || 1
  let acc = 0
  const rows: ReconRow[] = cats.map((k, i) => {
    const cat = s.categories.find((c) => c.id === k)
    const v = i === cats.length - 1 ? toSpread - acc : Math.round((toSpread * w[i]) / W)
    acc += v
    return { categoryId: k, name: cat?.name ?? k, element: cat?.erpCostElement ?? '—', budget: byCat[k], actual: v }
  })
  return {
    rows,
    unmapped: unmappedAmt ? { amount: unmappedAmt, period: last!.period, element: '5999000', text: `Posting to WBS ${p.erpWbs}.99 on suspense element 5999000 (simulated demo line)` } : null,
    erpTotal: pos.actualToDate,
    mainlineTotal: sum(periods.map((x) => x.actual)),
    budgetTotal: sum(Object.values(byCat)),
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Evidence completeness (RC-01, AI-06 style deterministic)
export interface Check { id: string; area: string; label: string; ok: boolean; severity: 'major' | 'minor'; gap?: string; tab?: string }
export interface Assessment { checks: Check[]; score: number; gaps: Check[]; passed: number }

export function assessEvidence(s: AppState, pid: string): Assessment {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)!
  const checks: Check[] = []
  const add = (c: Check) => checks.push(c)
  const pos = position(s, pid)
  const budget = activeBudget(s, pid)
  const t = s.settings.varianceThresholds

  // Need & driver
  add({ id: 'need-driver', area: 'Need', label: 'Regulatory driver and recovery proceeding named', ok: !!p.regulatoryCitation && !!p.fundingProceeding, severity: 'major', gap: 'Regulatory driver or proceeding not recorded', tab: 'overview' })
  add({ id: 'need-option', area: 'Need', label: 'Option analysis decision logged', ok: s.decisions.some((x) => x.projectId === pid), severity: 'minor', gap: 'No alternatives decision in the decision log', tab: 'records' })

  // Estimates
  const ests =s.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version)
  for (const e of ests.filter((e) => e.status === 'approved' || e.status === 'superseded')) {
    const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === e.workflowInstanceId)
    add({ id: `est-appr-${e.id}`, area: 'Estimates', label: `Estimate v${e.version} (Class ${e.cls}) approved through workflow`, ok: !!e.approvedById && inst?.status === 'approved', severity: 'major', gap: `Estimate v${e.version} has no workflow approval record`, tab: 'estimates' })
    const miss = [!e.basis.exclusions.length && 'exclusions', !e.basis.assumptions.length && 'assumptions', !e.basis.contingencyRationale.trim() && 'contingency rationale', !e.basis.classJustification.trim() && 'class justification', !e.basis.scopeBasis.trim() && 'scope basis'].filter(Boolean)
    add({ id: `est-boe-${e.id}`, area: 'Estimates', label: `Estimate v${e.version} basis of estimate complete`, ok: !miss.length, severity: 'minor', gap: `Estimate v${e.version} missing BOE ${miss.join(', ')}`, tab: 'estimates' })
  }
  for (const e of ests.filter((e) => e.status === 'in-review')) add({ id: `est-rev-${e.id}`, area: 'Estimates', label: `Estimate v${e.version} decided`, ok: false, severity: 'minor', gap: `Class ${e.cls} estimate v${e.version} is still in review`, tab: 'estimates' })
  if (budget) add({ id: 'bud-src', area: 'Budget', label: `${budget.kind === 'control' ? 'Control' : 'Planning'} budget traces to an approved estimate`, ok: ests.some((e) => e.id === budget.estimateId && (e.status === 'approved' || e.status === 'superseded')), severity: 'major', gap: 'Budget is not linked to an approved estimate', tab: 'estimates' })

  // Baselines
  const snaps = snapshotsFor(s, pid)
  if (budget?.kind === 'control') add({ id: 'bl-orig', area: 'Baselines', label: 'Original baseline frozen at authorization', ok: snaps.some((x) => x.type === 'original'), severity: 'major', gap: 'No Original baseline snapshot', tab: 'baselines' })
  for (const sn of snaps.filter((x) => x.type === 'current' || x.type === 'original')) {
    const a = snapshotAuthority(s, sn)
    add({ id: `bl-auth-${sn.id}`, area: 'Baselines', label: `${SNAP_META[sn.type].label} baseline ${fmtDate(sn.date)} has an approving decision`, ok: a.kind === 'Gate decision' || a.kind === 'Approved change', severity: 'major', gap: `${SNAP_META[sn.type].label} baseline of ${fmtDate(sn.date)} has no approving decision`, tab: 'baselines' })
  }

  // Changes
  const changes = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid)
  const variances = s.variances.filter((v) => v.projectId === pid)
  for (const c of changes.filter((c) => c.status === 'approved')) {
    const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === c.workflowInstanceId)
    const signed = !!inst && inst.history.some((h) => h.action === 'approve' && h.nodeId === 'approve' && h.signature)
    add({ id: `ch-sig-${c.id}`, area: 'Changes', label: `${c.code} approved by dollar authority with e-signature`, ok: signed, severity: 'major', gap: `${c.code} has no signed dollar-authority approval`, tab: 'changes' })
    add({ id: `ch-snap-${c.id}`, area: 'Changes', label: `${c.code} produced a new Current baseline`, ok: !!c.resultingSnapshotId && s.snapshots.some((x) => x.id === c.resultingSnapshotId), severity: 'major', gap: `${c.code} did not produce a baseline snapshot`, tab: 'changes' })
    if (Math.abs(c.costImpact) >= t.eacAbs || c.rootCause === 'Estimate error') add({ id: `ch-var-${c.id}`, area: 'Changes', label: `${c.code} (${money(c.costImpact)}) cited in a variance explanation`, ok: variances.some((v) => v.changeIds.includes(c.id) && v.status !== 'required'), severity: 'major', gap: `Change ${c.code} (${money(c.costImpact)}) has no linked variance explanation`, tab: 'cost' })
    if (c.fundedFrom === 'contingency') add({ id: `ch-risk-${c.id}`, area: 'Changes', label: `${c.code} contingency draw tied to a risk`, ok: !!c.riskId, severity: 'minor', gap: `${c.code} drew ${money(c.costImpact)} contingency without a linked risk`, tab: 'changes' })
  }

  // Variances
  const breaches = p.status === 'active' ? thresholdBreaches(s, p) : []
  for (const b of breaches) add({ id: `br-${b.description}`, area: 'Variances', label: `Threshold breach explained: ${b.description}`, ok: variances.some((v) => v.description === b.description && v.status !== 'required' && v.status !== 'returned'), severity: 'major', gap: `Unexplained threshold breach: ${b.description}`, tab: 'cost' })
  for (const v of variances.filter((v) => !breaches.some((b) => b.description === v.description))) {
    add({ id: `var-${v.id}`, area: 'Variances', label: `Variance "${v.description}" accepted`, ok: v.status === 'accepted', severity: v.status === 'submitted' ? 'minor' : 'major', gap: v.status === 'submitted' ? `Variance "${v.description}" awaiting acceptance` : `Variance "${v.description}" has no accepted explanation`, tab: 'cost' })
  }

  // Forecast
  if (budget?.kind === 'control' && p.status === 'active') {
    const fc = s.forecasts.filter((f) => f.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date)).pop()
    const age = fc ? daysBetween(fc.date, s.settings.asOf) : 999
    add({ id: 'fc-cur', area: 'Cost', label: 'Signed forecast within the last 45 days', ok: !!fc && fc.locked && age <= 45, severity: 'minor', gap: fc ? (fc.locked ? `Latest signed forecast is ${age} days old` : `Forecast of ${fmtDate(fc.date)} awaits PM sign-off`) : 'No forecast on record', tab: 'cost' })
  }

  // Risk & contingency
  for (const r of s.risks.filter((r) => r.projectId === pid && r.status === 'realized')) {
    const draw = s.contingencyDraws.find((d) => d.riskId === r.id && d.status === 'approved') || changes.find((c) => c.riskId === r.id && c.status === 'approved')
    add({ id: `rk-${r.id}`, area: 'Risk & contingency', label: `Realized risk ${r.code} funded by an approved draw or change`, ok: !!draw, severity: 'minor', gap: `Realized risk ${r.code} has no approved contingency draw`, tab: 'risk' })
  }
  for (const d of s.contingencyDraws.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid && x.status === 'approved')) {
    const inst = s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === d.workflowInstanceId)
    add({ id: `dr-${d.id}`, area: 'Risk & contingency', label: `Contingency draw ${money(d.amount)} approved through workflow`, ok: inst?.status === 'approved', severity: 'major', gap: `Contingency draw of ${money(d.amount)} (${fmtDate(d.date)}) has no workflow approval`, tab: 'risk' })
  }
  if (pos.contingencyOriginal) add({ id: 'cont-neg', area: 'Risk & contingency', label: 'Contingency not overdrawn', ok: pos.contingencyRemaining >= 0, severity: 'major', gap: `Contingency overdrawn by ${money(-pos.contingencyRemaining)}`, tab: 'risk' })

  // Gates
  for (const g of s.gateDecisions.filter((g) => g.projectId === pid)) {
    add({ id: `gd-${g.id}`, area: 'Gates', label: `${g.gateName} decision recorded with deciders and snapshot`, ok: g.decidedBy.length > 0 && !!g.snapshotId, severity: 'major', gap: `${g.gateName} decision lacks deciders or snapshot`, tab: 'lifecycle' })
    for (const aid of g.conditions) {
      const a = s.actions.find((x) => x.id === aid)
      if (a && a.status === 'open' && a.due < s.settings.asOf) add({ id: `gc-${aid}`, area: 'Gates', label: `Gate condition closed: ${a.title}`, ok: false, severity: 'minor', gap: `${g.gateName} condition "${a.title}" open past due (${fmtDate(a.due)})`, tab: 'lifecycle' })
    }
  }
  for (const d of s.deliverables.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid && x.status === 'waived')) add({ id: `wv-${d.id}`, area: 'Gates', label: 'Deliverable waiver', ok: false, severity: 'minor', gap: `Deliverable waived: ${d.waiverReason || 'no reason recorded'}`, tab: 'lifecycle' })

  // Records
  const recs = s.records.filter((r) => r.projectId === pid)
  if (recs.length) {
    const missing = recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'missing')
    add({ id: 'rec-missing', area: 'Records', label: 'Construction records attached (traceable)', ok: !missing.length, severity: 'major', gap: `${missing.length} of ${recs.length} construction records missing (${missing.slice(0, 2).map((r) => `${r.type}, ${r.segment}`).join('; ')}${missing.length > 2 ? '…' : ''})`, tab: 'records' })
    const unverified = recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'attached')
    add({ id: 'rec-verified', area: 'Records', label: 'Attached records verified', ok: !unverified.length, severity: 'minor', gap: `${unverified.length} attached records not yet verified`, tab: 'records' })
  }

  // Safety
  const plans = s.safetyPlans.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid)
  if (plans.length && p.status === 'active') add({ id: 'sf-plan', area: 'Safety', label: 'Approved job-specific safety plan in force', ok: plans.some((x) => x.status === 'approved'), severity: 'major', gap: 'No approved job-specific safety plan', tab: 'safety' })
  const sif = s.safetyEvents.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid && e.sifPotential && e.status === 'open')
  if (sif.length) add({ id: 'sf-sif', area: 'Safety', label: 'SIF-potential events closed', ok: false, severity: 'minor', gap: `${sif.length} SIF-potential event${sif.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} still open`, tab: 'safety' })

  const w = (c: Check) => (c.severity === 'major' ? 3 : 1)
  const total = sum(checks.map(w))
  const got = sum(checks.filter((c) => c.ok).map(w))
  const gaps = checks.filter((c) => !c.ok).sort((a, b) => (a.severity === b.severity ? 0 : a.severity === 'major' ? -1 : 1))
  return { checks, gaps, passed: checks.length - gaps.length, score: total ? Math.round((got / total) * 100) : 100 }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Small helpers
export function evmFor(s: AppState, pid: string) {
  const periods = s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid)
  return evmMetrics(periods, position(s, pid).baseBudget)
}

export const nextChangeCode = (s: AppState, p: Project) => `CR-${p.code.slice(4)}-${String(s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === p.id).length + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`

export const esc = (x: unknown) => String(x ?? '').replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;').replace(/"/g, '&quot;')

import { projectHref, go } from '@/core/router'
// Cost & forecast: EVM from monthly cost periods, monthly forecast cycle with PM sign-off (CC-02),
// variance explanations with AI-drafted narrative (BL-05/06/11), ERP reconciliation (CC-01).
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { mutate, useStore, personName } from '@/core/store'
import type { AppState, Forecast, RootCause, Variance } from '@/core/types'
import { ROOT_CAUSES } from '@/core/types'

import { latestOf, position, thresholdBreaches } from '@/core/engine/position'
import { actOnWorkflow, startWorkflow } from '@/core/engine/workflow'
import { ask, aiMessage, useAiAvailable } from '@/core/ai'

const short = (k: string) => fmtMonth(k).replace(/ (\d\d)(\d\d)$/, " '$2")

export default function Cost({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const pos = useMemo(() => position(s, projectId), [s, projectId])
  const periods = useMemo(() => s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId).sort((a, b) => a.period.localeCompare(b.period)), [s.costPeriods, projectId])
  const series = useMemo(() => evmSeries(periods), [periods])
  const m = useMemo(() => evmMetrics(periods, pos.baseBudget), [periods, pos.baseBudget])
  const [fcOpen, setFcOpen] = useState(false)
  const [varOpen, setVarOpen] = useState<VarTarget | null>(null)

  if (!periods.length && !pos.currentBudget) {
    return <Empty title="No cost history yet">{p.code} has no approved budget or ERP actuals. Cost periods start posting once the planning budget is authorized at Gate 1.</Empty>
  }
  const approvedChanges = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId && c.status === 'approved' && c.decidedAt)
  const markers = Array.from(new Map(approvedChanges.map((c) => [series.findIndex((x) => x.period === c.decidedAt!.slice(0, 7)), c] as const)).entries()).filter(([i]) => i >= 0).map(([i, c]) => ({ index: i, label: c.code.replace(/^CR-\d+-/, 'CR-') }))

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-8">
        <Stat label="CPI" value={m.cpi.toFixed(2)} tone={m.cpi < 0.95 ? 'bad' : m.cpi < 1 ? 'warn' : 'good'} sub={`CV ${moneyDelta(m.cv)}`} />
        <Stat label="SPI" value={m.spi.toFixed(2)} tone={m.spi < 0.95 ? 'bad' : m.spi < 1 ? 'warn' : 'good'} sub={`SV ${moneyDelta(m.sv)}`} />
        <Stat label="% complete" value={pct(m.pctComplete)} sub={`EV ${money(m.ev)} of BAC`} />
        <Stat label="BAC" value={money(m.bac)} sub="Budget excl. contingency left" />
        <Stat label="EAC (CPI method)" value={money(m.eac)} tone={m.eac > m.bac ? 'bad' : undefined} sub={`ETC ${money(m.etc)}`} />
        <Stat label="VAC" value={moneyDelta(m.vac)} tone={m.vac < 0 ? 'bad' : 'good'} />
        <Stat label="TCPI" value={m.tcpi.toFixed(2)} tone={m.tcpi > 1.1 ? 'bad' : m.tcpi > 1 ? 'warn' : undefined} sub="Efficiency to finish on BAC" />
        <Stat label="Signed EAC" value={money(pos.eac)} sub={`vs budget ${moneyDelta(pos.currentBudget - pos.eac)}`} tone={pos.eac > pos.currentBudget ? 'bad' : undefined} />
      </div>

      <Panel title="Earned value S-curve" subtitle={`Cumulative planned, earned and actual cost from ${periods.length} ERP periods · dashed lines: BAC and signed forecast EAC · markers: approved changes`}>
        {series.length > 1 ? (
          <LineChart labels={series.map((x) => short(x.period))} markers={markers} height={260} series={[
            { name: 'Planned (PV)', values: series.map((x) => x.cumPv), color: 'rgb(var(--muted))' },
            { name: 'Earned (EV)', values: series.map((x) => x.cumEv), color: 'rgb(var(--good))' },
            { name: 'Actual (AC)', values: series.map((x) => x.cumAc), color: 'rgb(var(--link))', area: true },
            { name: 'BAC', values: series.map(() => m.bac), color: 'rgb(var(--ink))', dashed: true },
            { name: 'EAC (signed)', values: series.map(() => pos.eac), color: 'rgb(var(--bad))', dashed: true },
          ]} />
        ) : <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">One period posted so far.</p>}
      </Panel>

      <VariancePanel s={s} projectId={projectId} onWrite={setVarOpen} />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <ForecastPanel s={s} projectId={projectId} onNew={() => go(projectHref(projectId, 'forecast'))} />
        <ReconPanel s={s} projectId={projectId} />
      </div>

      <Panel title="Monthly cost periods" subtitle={`ERP WBS ${p.erpWbs} · values for each month (not cumulative)`} pad={false}>
        <Table rows={[...series].reverse()} rowKey={(x) => x.period} maxHeight={420} dense columns={[
          { key: 'p', header: 'Period', render: (x) => fmtMonth(x.period), sort: (x) => x.period },
          { key: 'pv', header: 'Planned', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.pv, 'full') },
          { key: 'ev', header: 'Earned', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.ev, 'full') },
          { key: 'ac', header: 'Actual', num: true, render: (x) => <span className={x.ac > x.ev * 1.08 ? 'text-bad' : ''}>{money(x.ac, 'full')}</span> },
          { key: 'cm', header: 'Commitments', num: true, render: (x) => money(periods.find((c) => c.period === x.period)?.commitments, 'full') },
          { key: 'cac', header: 'Cum. actual', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.cumAc) },
          { key: 'cpi', header: 'Cum. CPI', num: true, render: (x) => (x.cumAc ? (x.cumEv / x.cumAc).toFixed(2) : '—') },
        ]} />
      </Panel>

      <ForecastModal open={fcOpen} onClose={() => setFcOpen(false)} projectId={projectId} />
      {varOpen && <VarianceDrawer key={varOpen.id ?? varOpen.breach?.description} projectId={projectId} varianceId={varOpen.id} breach={varOpen.breach} preChanges={varOpen.changeIds} onClose={() => setVarOpen(null)} />}
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Variances
type VarTarget = { id?: string; breach?: { metric: 'eac' | 'milestone'; description: string; amount: number }; changeIds?: string[] }
function VariancePanel({ s, projectId, onWrite }: { s: AppState; projectId: string; onWrite: (x: VarTarget) => void }) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const variances = s.variances.filter((v) => v.projectId === projectId).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))
  const breaches = p.status === 'active' ? thresholdBreaches(s, p) : []
  const unexplained = breaches.filter((b) => !variances.some((v) => v.description === b.description))
  const t = s.settings.varianceThresholds
  const cur = latestOf(s, projectId, 'current') ?? latestOf(s, projectId, 'original')
  const pos = position(s, projectId)
  const unexplainedChanges = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId && c.status === 'approved' && (Math.abs(c.costImpact) >= t.eacAbs || c.rootCause === 'Estimate error') && !variances.some((v) => v.changeIds.includes(c.id) && v.status !== 'required'))
  const [pick, setPick] = useState('')
  const explain = () => { const c = unexplainedChanges.find((x) => x.id === (pick || unexplainedChanges[0]?.id)); if (c) onWrite({ breach: { metric: 'eac', description: `${c.code} cost growth — ${c.title}`, amount: c.costImpact }, changeIds: [c.id] }) }
  return (
    <Panel title="Variance explanations" subtitle={`Thresholds: EAC above Current baseline by more than ${t.eacPct}% or ${money(t.eacAbs)}; milestone slip over ${t.milestoneDays} days`}
      actions={unexplainedChanges.length ? <><Select className="w-auto max-w-[260px]" value={pick || unexplainedChanges[0].id} onChange={(e) => setPick(e.target.value)} options={unexplainedChanges.map((c) => ({ value: c.id, label: `${c.code} · ${moneyDelta(c.costImpact)}` }))} /><Button size="sm" onClick={explain}>Explain change</Button></> : undefined}>
      {unexplainedChanges.length > 0 && <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-muted">{unexplainedChanges.length} approved change{unexplainedChanges.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} of {money(t.eacAbs)} or more {unexplainedChanges.length > 1 ? 'have' : 'has'} no variance narrative; reviewers expect one for material cost growth.</p>}
      {unexplained.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
          {unexplained.map((b) => (
            <div key={b.description} className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-md border border-marker bg-marker/10 px-3 py-2">
              <div className="min-w-0 text-[13px]"><Badge tone="bad">Breach</Badge> <b>{b.description}</b> <span className="text-muted">· {b.metric === 'eac' ? moneyDelta(b.amount) : `${b.amount} days`} · explanation required before period close</span></div>
              <Button size="sm" variant="marker" onClick={() => onWrite({ breach: b })}>Write explanation</Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {variances.length ? (
        <Table rows={variances} rowKey={(v) => v.id} onRowClick={(v) => onWrite({ id: v.id })} dense columns={[
          { key: 'd', header: 'Date', render: (v) => fmtDate(v.date), width: 110 },
          { key: 'm', header: 'Metric', render: (v) => <Badge>{v.metric === 'eac' ? 'EAC' : 'Milestone'}</Badge> },
          { key: 'x', header: 'What crossed the threshold', render: (v) => <span className="block min-w-[220px]">{v.description}{v.narrative && <span className="mt-0.5 block line-clamp-2 text-xs text-muted">{v.narrative}</span>}</span> },
          { key: 'a', header: 'Amount', num: true, render: (v) => (v.metric === 'eac' ? moneyDelta(v.amount) : `${v.amount} d`) },
          { key: 'r', header: 'Root cause', render: (v) => v.rootCause ?? <span className="text-faint">—</span> },
          { key: 'l', header: 'Linked', render: (v) => <span className="text-xs text-muted">{v.changeIds.length} chg · {v.riskIds.length} risk</span> },
          { key: 's', header: 'Status', render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v.status} /> },
        ]} />
      ) : !unexplained.length ? (
        <Callout tone="good" title="Within thresholds">
          {cur ? <>Signed EAC {money(pos.eac)} is {moneyDelta(pos.eac - cur.budget.total)} against the Current baseline of {money(cur.budget.total)} ({fmtDate(cur.date)}); no milestone has slipped more than {t.milestoneDays} days. No explanation is due.</> : 'No Current baseline yet — thresholds apply once the control budget is authorized.'}
        </Callout>
      ) : null}
    </Panel>
  )
}

function VarianceDrawer({ projectId, varianceId, breach, preChanges, onClose }: { projectId: string; varianceId?: string; breach?: { metric: 'eac' | 'milestone'; description: string; amount: number }; preChanges?: string[]; onClose: () => void }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const ai = useAiAvailable()
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const v = s.variances.find((x) => x.id === varianceId)
  const subject = v ?? (breach ? { metric: breach.metric, description: breach.description, amount: breach.amount } : undefined)
  const editable = !v || v.status === 'required' || v.status === 'returned'
  const [rootCause, setRootCause] = useState<RootCause>(v?.rootCause ?? 'Productivity')
  const [narrative, setNarrative] = useState(v?.narrative ?? '')
  const [changeIds, setChangeIds] = useState<string[]>(v?.changeIds ?? preChanges ?? [])
  const [riskIds, setRiskIds] = useState<string[]>(v?.riskIds ?? [])
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [aiDraft, setAiDraft] = useState(false)
  if (!subject) return null
  const changes = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId && (c.status === 'approved' || c.status === 'in-review'))
  const risks = s.risks.filter((r) => r.projectId === projectId && (r.status === 'realized' || r.status === 'open' || r.status === 'mitigating'))
  const pos = position(s, projectId)
  const cur = latestOf(s, projectId, 'current') ?? latestOf(s, projectId, 'original')
  const periods = s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId)
  const m = evmMetrics(periods, pos.baseBudget)
  const toggle = (xs: string[], id: string) => (xs.includes(id) ? xs.filter((x) => x !== id) : [...xs, id])

  const draft = async () => {
    setBusy(true); setAiDraft(false); setNarrative('Thinking…')
    const linked = changes.filter((c) => c.status === 'approved')
    const realized = s.risks.filter((r) => r.projectId === projectId && r.status === 'realized')
    const recent = periods.slice().sort((a, b) => a.period.localeCompare(b.period)).slice(-4)
    const slips = p.milestones.filter((x) => !x.actual).map((x) => { const b = cur?.milestones.find((y) => y.name === x.name); return b ? `${x.name}: baseline-current ${b.forecast}, forecast ${x.forecast} (${daysBetween(b.forecast, x.forecast)} days)` : '' }).filter(Boolean)
    const prompt = `You are drafting a variance explanation for a gas utility capital project. It will be reviewed by project controls and a program manager and may be produced in a CPUC reasonableness review, so state facts plainly and do not speculate beyond the data.

Project: ${p.code} ${p.name} (${p.workMethod.replace(/-/g, ' ')}, ${p.location.city}, ${p.location.terrain}). Contractor: ${p.contractor ?? 'not recorded'}.
Breach: ${subject.description}. Amount: ${subject.metric === 'eac' ? money(subject.amount, 'full') : subject.amount + ' days'}.
Current baseline ${cur ? `${money(cur.budget.total, 'full')} (${cur.label}, ${cur.date})` : 'none'}; signed EAC ${money(pos.eac, 'full')}; actual to date ${money(pos.actualToDate, 'full')}; contingency remaining ${money(pos.contingencyRemaining, 'full')} of ${money(pos.contingencyOriginal, 'full')}.
EVM: CPI ${m.cpi.toFixed(2)}, SPI ${m.spi.toFixed(2)}, ${pct(m.pctComplete)} complete, CPI-based EAC ${money(m.eac, 'full')}.
Last periods (planned/earned/actual): ${recent.map((r) => `${r.period} ${money(r.planned)}/${money(r.earned)}/${money(r.actual)}`).join('; ')}.
Approved changes: ${linked.map((c) => `${c.code} "${c.title}" ${money(c.costImpact)} ${c.rootCause}, ${c.scheduleImpactDays} days, decided ${c.decidedAt}`).join('; ') || 'none'}.
Realized risks: ${realized.map((r) => `${r.code} "${r.title}" most-likely ${money(r.cost.ml)}`).join('; ') || 'none'}.
Milestones: ${slips.join('; ') || 'none'}.
Root cause selected by the analyst: ${rootCause}.

Write the narrative only: 3 to 5 sentences, plain prose, no headings or bullets. Cover what happened, the cost or schedule effect with figures, the corrective action, and the expected trend. Cite change codes where relevant.`
    try {
      const text = await ask(prompt, { onText: setNarrative })
      setNarrative(text.trim()); setAiDraft(true)
    } catch (e) { setNarrative(v?.narrative ?? ''); toast(aiMessage(e), 'bad') } finally { setBusy(false) }
  }

  const submit = () => {
    if (narrative.trim().length < 40) { toast('Write a narrative of at least a couple of sentences.', 'bad'); return }
    const me = s.currentUserId
    let autoSubmitted = false
    mutate((d) => {
      let rec = d.variances.find((x) => x.id === varianceId)
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { id: uid('var'), projectId, date: d.settings.asOf, metric: subject.metric, description: subject.description, amount: subject.amount, narrative: '', status: 'required', changeIds: [], riskIds: [] } as Variance
        d.variances.push(rec)
      }
      Object.assign(rec, { rootCause, narrative: narrative.trim(), changeIds, riskIds, authorId: me })
      const inst = startWorkflow(d, 'variance-explanation', { title: `${p.code} variance: ${rec.description}`, recordType: 'variance', recordId: rec.id, projectId, data: { rootCause, narrative: narrative.trim(), amount: rec.metric === 'eac' ? rec.amount : 0 } })
      const isAdmin = d.people.find((x) => x.id === me)?.roles.includes('admin')
      const slot = inst?.active.find((a) => a.assigneeIds.includes(me)) ?? (isAdmin ? inst?.active[0] : undefined)
      if (inst && slot) { actOnWorkflow(d, inst.id, slot.nodeId, me, 'submit', 'Explanation written'); autoSubmitted = true }
    }, { entity: 'variance', entityId: varianceId ?? subject.description, projectId, action: 'submit', summary: `Submitted variance explanation: ${subject.description} (${rootCause})` })
    toast(autoSubmitted ? 'Submitted for controls validation' : `Saved — waiting on ${personName(s, p.team.pm)} to submit`)
    onClose()
  }

  return (
    <Drawer open onClose={onClose} width={620} title="Variance explanation"
      footer={editable ? <><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" onClick={submit} disabled={busy}>Submit explanation</Button></> : <Button onClick={onClose}>Close</Button>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Callout tone={v?.status === 'accepted' ? 'good' : 'marker'} title={subject.description}>
          {subject.metric === 'eac' ? `${moneyDelta(subject.amount)} ${preChanges?.length ? "of approved cost growth" : "over the Current baseline"}` : `${subject.amount} days of slip`}. Current baseline {cur ? `${money(cur.budget.total)} (${fmtDate(cur.date)})` : '—'} · signed EAC {money(pos.eac)} · CPI {m.cpi.toFixed(2)} · SPI {m.spi.toFixed(2)}.
        </Callout>
        {v?.workflowInstanceId && <WorkflowStatus instanceId={v.workflowInstanceId} />}
        {v?.status === 'returned' && <Callout tone="bad" title="Returned for revision">Revise the narrative and resubmit; the reviewer's comment is in the workflow history above.</Callout>}
        <Field label="Root cause"><Select disabled={!editable} value={rootCause} onChange={(e) => setRootCause(e.target.value as RootCause)} options={[...ROOT_CAUSES]} /></Field>
        <Field label={<span className="flex items-center gap-2">Narrative {aiDraft && <AiTag />}</span>} hint={aiDraft ? 'AI drafted from the figures above. Edit it until it is yours; it is submitted under your name.' : 'What happened, the effect in dollars or days, the corrective action, and the expected trend.'}>
          <TextArea disabled={!editable || busy} className="min-h-[140px]" value={narrative} onChange={(e) => { setNarrative(e.target.value); setAiDraft(false) }} />
        </Field>
        {editable && ai !== false && <div><Button size="sm" onClick={draft} disabled={busy || ai === null}>{busy ? 'Thinking…' : 'AI draft narrative'}</Button> <span className="text-xs text-faint">Uses the breach, approved changes, realized risks and cost periods.</span></div>}
        <Section title="Linked changes">
          {changes.length ? changes.map((c) => <Checkbox key={c.id} disabled={!editable} checked={changeIds.includes(c.id)} onChange={() => setChangeIds(toggle(changeIds, c.id))} label={<span><Mono>{c.code}</Mono> {c.title} <span className="text-muted">· {moneyDelta(c.costImpact)} · {c.status}</span></span>} />) : <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">No changes on this project.</p>}
        </Section>
        <Section title="Linked risks">
          {risks.length ? risks.map((r) => <Checkbox key={r.id} disabled={!editable} checked={riskIds.includes(r.id)} onChange={() => setRiskIds(toggle(riskIds, r.id))} label={<span><Mono>{r.code}</Mono> {r.title} <span className="text-muted">· {r.status} · ML {money(r.cost.ml)}</span></span>} />) : <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">No open or realized risks.</p>}
        </Section>
      </div>
    </Drawer>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Forecast cycle (CC-02)
function ForecastPanel({ s, projectId, onNew }: { s: AppState; projectId: string; onNew: () => void }) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const me = s.people.find((x) => x.id === s.currentUserId)!
  const fcs = s.forecasts.filter((f) => f.projectId === projectId).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date))
  const cycle = s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 7)
  const thisCycle = fcs.find((f) => f.date.slice(0, 7) === cycle)
  const canSign = me.id === p.team.pm || me.roles.includes('admin')
  const sign = (f: Forecast) => {
    mutate((d) => { const x = d.forecasts.find((y) => y.id === f.id); if (x) x.locked = true }, { entity: 'forecast', entityId: f.id, projectId, action: 'sign-off', summary: `PM sign-off locked ${fmtMonth(f.date.slice(0, 7))} forecast EAC ${money(f.eac, 'full')}${me.id !== p.team.pm ? ' (as administrator)' : ''}` })
    toast('Forecast signed and locked')
  }
  const signer = (f: Forecast) => [...s.audit].reverse().find((a) => a.entity === 'forecast' && a.entityId === f.id && a.action === 'sign-off')
  return (
    <Panel title="Forecast history" subtitle={`Monthly cycle · ${fmtMonth(cycle)} ${thisCycle ? (thisCycle.locked ? 'signed' : 'awaiting PM sign-off') : 'not started'}`}
      actions={<Button size="sm" variant={thisCycle ? 'secondary' : 'marker'} onClick={onNew} icon={<Icon name="plus" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}>Spread forecast by WBS</Button>} pad={false}>
      {fcs.length ? (
        <Table rows={fcs} rowKey={(f) => f.id} dense columns={[
          { key: 'd', header: 'Date', render: (f) => fmtDate(f.date) },
          { key: 'e', header: 'EAC', num: true, render: (f) => money(f.eac, 'full') },
          { key: 'c', header: 'Change', num: true, render: (f) => { const prev = fcs[fcs.indexOf(f) + 1]; return prev ? <span className={f.eac > prev.eac ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good'}>{moneyDelta(f.eac - prev.eac)}</span> : '—' } },
          { key: 'y', header: 'By year', render: (f) => <span className="text-xs text-muted">{Object.entries(f.byYear).map(([y, v]) => `${y} ${money(v)}`).join(' · ')}</span> },
          { key: 'n', header: 'Note', render: (f) => <span className="block min-w-[140px] text-xs">{f.note}<span className="block text-faint">by {personName(s, f.byId)}</span></span> },
          { key: 'l', header: 'Sign-off', render: (f) => (f.locked ? <span title={signer(f) ? `Signed by ${personName(s, signer(f)!.actorId)}` : 'Signed in the forecast cycle'}><Badge tone="good">Locked</Badge></span> : canSign ? <Button size="sm" variant="marker" onClick={() => sign(f)}>{me.id === p.team.pm ? 'Sign & lock' : 'Sign as admin'}</Button> : <Badge tone="marker">Awaiting {personName(s, p.team.pm)}</Badge>) },
        ]} />
      ) : <div className="p-4"><Empty title="No forecasts yet">Start the first forecast cycle for this project.</Empty></div>}
    </Panel>
  )
}

function ForecastModal({ open, onClose, projectId }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; projectId: string }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const pos = position(s, projectId)
  const last = s.forecasts.filter((f) => f.projectId === projectId).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date)).pop()
  const y0 = Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))
  const endY = Math.max(y0, ...Object.keys(last?.byYear ?? {}).map(Number), Number((p.milestones.find((m) => /in.service/i.test(m.name))?.forecast ?? s.settings.asOf).slice(0, 4)))
  const years = Array.from({ length: endY - y0 + 1 }, (_, i) => String(y0 + i))
  const init = () => { const base = last?.byYear ?? {}; const W = sum(years.map((y) => base[y] ?? 0)) || 1; return Object.fromEntries(years.map((y) => [y, Math.round(((base[y] ?? 1 / years.length) / W) * (last?.eac ?? pos.eac))])) as Record<string, number> }
  const [eac, setEac] = useState(last?.eac ?? pos.eac)
  const [byYear, setByYear] = useState<Record<string, number>>(init)
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const total = sum(Object.values(byYear))
  const rescale = () => { const W = total || 1; let acc = 0; setByYear(Object.fromEntries(years.map((y, i) => { const v = i === years.length - 1 ? eac - acc : Math.round((byYear[y] / W) * eac); acc += v; return [y, v] }))) }
  const submit = () => {
    if (!note.trim()) { toast('Add a forecast note (what moved and why).', 'bad'); return }
    if (Math.abs(total - eac) > 1) { toast('The by-year spread must add up to the EAC.', 'bad'); return }
    mutate((d) => { d.forecasts.push({ id: uid('fc'), projectId, date: d.settings.asOf, eac, byYear, note: note.trim(), byId: d.currentUserId, locked: false }) }, { entity: 'forecast', entityId: projectId, projectId, action: 'create', summary: `Entered ${fmtMonth(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 7))} forecast EAC ${money(eac, 'full')} (${moneyDelta(eac - (last?.eac ?? 0))})` })
    toast(`Forecast submitted — ${personName(s, p.team.pm)} signs to lock it`)
    setNote(''); onClose()
  }
  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={`${fmtMonth(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 7))} forecast cycle`} footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" onClick={submit}>Submit forecast</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <KV cols={3} items={[['Prior signed EAC', money(last?.eac, 'full')], ['Actual to date', money(pos.actualToDate, 'full')], ['Current budget', money(pos.currentBudget, 'full')]]} />
        <Field label="Estimate at completion"><MoneyInput value={eac} onChange={setEac} /></Field>
        <Section title="Spread by year" actions={<Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={rescale}>Rescale to EAC</Button>}>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
            {years.map((y) => <Field key={y} label={y}><MoneyInput value={byYear[y] ?? 0} onChange={(v) => setByYear({ ...byYear, [y]: v })} /></Field>)}
          </div>
          <div className={cx('text-xs tabular-nums', Math.abs(total - eac) > 1 ? 'font-semibold text-bad' : 'text-muted')}>Spread total {money(total, 'full')} {Math.abs(total - eac) > 1 ? `· ${moneyDelta(total - eac)} vs EAC` : '· ties to EAC'}</div>
        </Section>
        <Field label="Forecast note"><TextArea value={note} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Segment B rock productivity carried to completion; CR-1001-02 assumed approved." /></Field>
        <p className="text-xs text-faint">The PM's sign-off locks the forecast. Locked forecasts cannot be edited; the next cycle creates a new version.</p>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── ERP reconciliation (CC-01)
function ReconPanel({ s, projectId }: { s: AppState; projectId: string }) {
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const r = useMemo(() => erpRecon(s, projectId), [s, projectId])
  const [mapTo, setMapTo] = useState(r.rows[0]?.categoryId ?? '')
  const mapLine = () => {
    const u = r.unmapped
    if (!u) return
    const cat = s.categories.find((c) => c.id === mapTo)
    mutate(() => {}, { entity: 'erp-recon', entityId: `${projectId}:${u.period}`, projectId, action: 'map', summary: `Mapped ${money(u.amount, 'full')} suspense posting (${u.period}) to ${cat?.code} ${cat?.name}` })
    toast('Posting mapped; journal correction requested')
  }
  const tie = r.erpTotal === r.mainlineTotal
  return (
    <Panel title="ERP reconciliation" subtitle={`WBS ${p.erpWbs} · actuals by cost element vs budget by category`} pad={false}>
      <Table rows={r.rows} rowKey={(x) => x.categoryId} dense maxHeight={300} columns={[
        { key: 'c', header: 'Category', render: (x) => <span>{x.name} <Mono className="text-faint">{x.element}</Mono></span> },
        { key: 'b', header: 'Budget', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.budget) },
        { key: 'a', header: 'Actual', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.actual) },
        { key: 'p', header: 'Spent', num: true, render: (x) => <span className={x.actual > x.budget ? 'font-semibold text-bad' : ''}>{x.budget ? pct(x.actual / x.budget) : '—'}</span>, sort: (x) => (x.budget ? x.actual / x.budget : 0) },
      ]} />
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-t border-line p-3 text-[13px]">
        {r.unmapped ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-md border border-marker bg-marker/10 p-2.5">
            <div><Badge tone="marker">Unmapped</Badge> <b className="tabular-nums">{money(r.unmapped.amount, 'full')}</b> in {fmtMonth(r.unmapped.period)} — {r.unmapped.text}.</div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><Select className="w-auto min-w-[200px]" value={mapTo} onChange={(e) => setMapTo(e.target.value)} options={r.rows.map((x) => ({ value: x.categoryId, label: x.name }))} /><Button size="sm" onClick={mapLine}>Map to category</Button></div>
          </div>
        ) : <div className="text-good">All postings mapped to cost categories.</div>}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 text-xs text-muted">
          <span>ERP actual {money(r.erpTotal, 'full')} · Crosswalk cost periods {money(r.mainlineTotal, 'full')} <Badge tone={tie ? 'good' : 'bad'}>{tie ? 'Ties' : 'Out of balance'}</Badge></span>
          <span>Budget by category {money(r.budgetTotal)} (excl. contingency)</span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[11px] text-faint">Cost-element detail is simulated from the period totals for this demo; totals come from the ERP cost periods.</p>
      </div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/Estimates.tsx =====
// Project estimates (Module 2): versions, the estimate builder, budgets created from estimates,
// version compare, and accuracy against actuals. ES-01..ES-13.
import { prefs } from '@/core/data'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import type { AppState, Estimate, Project } from '@/core/types'
import { getState, mutate, personName, useMe, useStore } from '@/core/store'
import { CLASS_RANGES, CONT_CAT, createBudgetFromEstimate, estimateBridge, estimateTotals } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { startWorkflow } from '@/core/engine/workflow'
import { WorkflowStatus } from '@/views/shared/WorkflowStatus'
import { activeBudget, position, projectBudgets } from '@/core/engine/position'
import { templateFor } from '@/core/engine/gates'
import { cx, fmtDate, money, moneyDelta, pct, sum } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, ClassBadge, Empty, Field, Modal, Panel, Select, StatusBadge, Table, Input, type Column } from '@/ui/kit'
import { RangeBar, Waterfall } from '@/ui/charts'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'
import { EstimateEditor } from '@/views/estimating/Editor'
import { AccPct, Legend, NumInput, OutcomeBar, Swatch } from '@/views/estimating/parts'
import { QuickEstimateView } from '@/views/estimating/quick/QuickView'
import { QuickRequest } from '@/views/estimating/quick/QuickForm'
import { QuickBadge } from '@/views/estimating/quick/parts'
import { quickEligibility } from '@/views/estimating/quick/lib'
import {
  accuracyForProject, budgetKindAvailable, buildFromExisting, buildParametric, catCode, catName, defaultInputs, defaultPackages, describeInputs, inputSources, latestEstimate,
  displayTitle, methodLabel, outcomeFor, projectEstimates, shortCat,
} from '@/views/estimating/lib'
import { pricedOnVersion, salesTaxFor } from '@/core/engine/estimatingAdmin'
import { projectHref } from '@/core/router'

const pickKey = (pid: string) => `crosswalk:est-sel:${pid}`

export default function Estimates({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const me = useMe()
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)
  const ests = useMemo(() => projectEstimates(s, projectId), [s.estimates, projectId]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const [sel, setSel] = useState<string | undefined>(() => { try { return prefs.getRaw(pickKey(projectId)) ?? undefined } catch { return undefined } })
  const [showNew5, setShowNew5] = useState(false)
  const [showQuick, setShowQuick] = useState(false)
  const [copyFrom, setCopyFrom] = useState<Estimate | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => { try { if (sel) prefs.setRaw(pickKey(projectId), sel) } catch { /* storage blocked */ } }, [sel, projectId])
  if (!p) return <></>
  const selected = ests.find((e) => e.id === sel) ?? ests.find((e) => e.status === 'draft') ?? ests.find((e) => e.status === 'in-review') ?? ests[ests.length - 1]
  const latest = latestEstimate(s, projectId)
  const hasDraft = ests.some((e) => e.status === 'draft')
  const canEstimate = me.roles.some((r) => ['estimator', 'estimating-lead', 'pmo', 'admin', 'project-controls', 'pm'].includes(r))
  const gate = templateFor(s, p).stages[p.stageIndex]?.gate
  const approved = ests.filter((e) => e.status === 'approved').pop()
  const quickOk = quickEligibility(s, p.id)
  const canRequest = !me.company
  const quickBtn = (primary?: boolean) => canRequest && <Button variant={primary ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} onClick={() => setShowQuick(true)} disabled={!quickOk.ok} title={quickOk.reason}>Request Class 5 quick estimate</Button>

  const createClass3 = () => {
    if (!latest) return
    const e = buildFromExisting(getState(), latest, me.id, { split: latest.method === 'parametric' ? defaultPackages(p) : undefined, toClass3: true })
    mutate((d) => { d.estimates.push(e) }, { entity: 'estimate', entityId: e.id, projectId: p.id, action: 'create', summary: `Created draft v${e.version} (Class 3 line-item) from v${latest.version}${latest.method === 'parametric' ? ` rate-table estimate, split into ${defaultPackages(p).join(', ')}` : ''}` })
    setSel(e.id)
    toast(`Draft v${e.version} created from v${latest.version}`)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      {/* summary strip */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:grid-cols-4">
        <Summary label="Estimate of record" value={approved ? money(estimateTotals(approved).total) : '—'} sub={approved ? <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"><ClassBadge cls={approved.cls} /> v{approved.version} · approved {fmtDate(approved.approvedAt?.slice(0, 10))}</span> : 'None approved yet'} />
        <Summary label="Expected range" value={approved ? `${money(estimateTotals(approved).low)}–${money(estimateTotals(approved).high)}` : '—'} sub={approved ? `AACE Class ${approved.cls}: ${pct(CLASS_RANGES[approved.cls].low)} / +${pct(CLASS_RANGES[approved.cls].high)}` : undefined} />
        <Summary label={activeBudget(s, p.id)?.kind === 'control' ? 'Control budget' : 'Planning budget'} value={money(activeBudget(s, p.id)?.total)} sub={activeBudget(s, p.id) ? `From estimate v${s.estimates.find((e) => e.id === activeBudget(s, p.id)?.estimateId)?.version ?? '—'}` : 'Created at the gate from an approved estimate'} />
        <Summary label="Gate requirement" value={gate?.requiredEstimateClass ? <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><ClassBadge cls={gate.requiredEstimateClass} size="lg" /><span className="text-[14px]">{approved && approved.cls <= gate.requiredEstimateClass ? 'Met' : 'Not met'}</span></span> : 'None'} sub={gate?.name ?? 'Final stage'} tone={gate?.requiredEstimateClass ? (approved && approved.cls <= gate.requiredEstimateClass ? 'good' : 'warn') : undefined} />
      </div>

      {/* versions */}
      <Panel pad={false} title="Estimate versions" subtitle="Approved and superseded versions are locked. Every change creates a new version."
        actions={<>
          {!canEstimate && quickBtn()}
          {canEstimate && <>{quickBtn()}
          <Button onClick={() => setShowNew5(true)} disabled={hasDraft} title={hasDraft ? 'Finish or submit the open draft first' : 'Class 5 priced from the rate table using the scope statement quantities'}>New Class 5 rate-table estimate</Button>
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={createClass3} disabled={!latest || hasDraft || latest.status === 'in-review'} title={hasDraft ? 'Finish or submit the open draft first' : !latest ? 'Create a Class 5 first' : latest.status === 'in-review' ? `Wait for v${latest.version} to finish review` : undefined}>New Class 3 from latest</Button>
          </>}
        </>}>
        {ests.length === 0 ? <div className="p-4"><Empty title="No estimates yet" action={<div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">{quickBtn(true)}{canEstimate && <Button onClick={() => setShowNew5(true)}>New Class 5 rate-table estimate</Button>}</div>}>Ask the estimating team for a historical quick estimate (a few dropdowns), or build a Class 5 rate-table estimate that prices the scope statement quantities from the rate table. The approved Class 5 becomes the planning budget at Gate 1.</Empty></div> : (
          <VersionTable s={s} ests={ests} selectedId={selected?.id} onSelect={(e) => setSel(e.id)} />
        )}
      </Panel>

      {selected && (selected.method === 'quick'
        ? <QuickEstimateView key={selected.id} estimate={selected} project={p} onCopy={(src) => setCopyFrom(src)} />
        : <EstimateEditor key={selected.id} estimate={selected} project={p} onCopy={(src) => setCopyFrom(src)} />)}

      <BudgetsPanel s={s} project={p} onOpenEstimate={(id) => { setSel(id); document.getElementById('main')?.scrollTo?.({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }) }} />
      {ests.length >= 2 && <ComparePanel s={s} ests={ests} />}
      <AccuracyPanel s={s} project={p} />

      {showNew5 && <NewParametric project={p} onClose={() => setShowNew5(false)} onCreated={(id) => { setSel(id); setShowNew5(false) }} />}
      {showQuick && (
        <Modal open wide onClose={() => setShowQuick(false)} title={`Request Class 5 quick estimate · ${p.code}`}>
          <QuickRequest projectId={p.id} onCancel={() => setShowQuick(false)} onDone={(id) => { setSel(id); setShowQuick(false) }} />
        </Modal>
      )}
      {copyFrom && <CopyModal project={p} src={copyFrom} onClose={() => setCopyFrom(null)} onCreated={(id) => { setSel(id); setCopyFrom(null) }} />}
    </div>
  )
}

function Summary({ label, value, sub, tone }: { label: string; value: React.ReactNode; sub?: React.ReactNode; tone?: 'good' | 'warn' }) {
  return (
    <div className={cx('min-w-0 rounded-md border bg-surface px-4 py-3', tone === 'warn' ? 'border-warn/50' : 'border-line')}>
      <div className="eyebrow truncate">{label}</div>
      <div className={cx('mt-1 font-display text-[21px] font-bold leading-tight tabular-nums', tone === 'good' && 'text-good', tone === 'warn' && 'text-warn')}>{value}</div>
      {sub && <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Version list
function VersionTable({ s, ests, selectedId, onSelect }: { s: AppState; ests: Estimate[]; selectedId?: string; onSelect: (e: Estimate) => void }) {
  const totals = ests.map((e) => estimateTotals(e))
  const scaleMax = Math.max(...totals.map((t) => t.high)) * 1.04
  const rows = ests.map((e, i) => ({ e, t: totals[i] })).reverse()
  const cols: Column<(typeof rows)[number]>[] = [
    { key: 'v', header: 'Ver', width: 54, sort: (r) => r.e.version, render: (r) => <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5"><span className={cx('h-4 w-1 rounded-full', r.e.id === selectedId ? 'bg-marker' : 'bg-transparent')} /><span className="font-mono font-semibold">v{r.e.version}</span></span> },
    { key: 'title', header: 'Title', render: (r) => <div><span className="font-semibold">{displayTitle(r.e)}</span>{r.e.repricedFrom && <div className="text-[11px] text-faint">Repriced from v{r.e.repricedFrom.fromVersion} to rate table v{r.e.repricedFrom.toRateTable}</div>}</div> },
    { key: 'cls', header: 'Class', sort: (r) => r.e.cls, render: (r) => <ClassBadge cls={r.e.cls} /> },
    { key: 'm', header: 'Method', render: (r) => r.e.method === 'quick' ? <QuickBadge /> : <span className="text-[12px] text-muted">{methodLabel(r.e.method)}<div className="text-[11px] text-faint">Rate table v{pricedOnVersion(s, r.e)}{pricedOnVersion(s, r.e) < s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion ? ' (older)' : ''}</div></span> },
    { key: 'st', header: 'Status', render: (r) => <StatusBadge status={r.e.status} /> },
    { key: 'tot', header: 'Total', num: true, sort: (r) => r.t.total, render: (r) => <b>{money(r.t.total)}</b> },
