    { key: 'rng', header: 'Range (low – high)', width: 230, render: (r) => <div className="min-w-[180px]"><RangeBar low={r.t.low} point={r.t.total} high={r.t.high} scaleMax={scaleMax} /><div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] tabular-nums text-faint"><span>{money(r.t.low)}</span><span>{money(r.t.high)}</span></div></div> },
    { key: 'by', header: 'Created by', render: (r) => <span className="text-[12px]">{personName(s, r.e.createdById)}<div className="text-faint">{fmtDate(r.e.createdAt.slice(0, 10))}</div></span> },
    { key: 'ap', header: 'Approved', render: (r) => r.e.approvedAt ? <span className="text-[12px]">{personName(s, r.e.approvedById)}<div className="text-faint">{fmtDate(r.e.approvedAt.slice(0, 10))}</div></span> : <span className="text-faint">—</span> },
  ]
  return <Table rows={rows} columns={cols} rowKey={(r) => r.e.id} onRowClick={(r) => onSelect(r.e)} />
}

// ───────────────────────────── New Class 5 rate-table estimate (ES-01)
/** Class 5 from the rate table: quantities, diameter and region are read from the scope statement and the project record. */
function NewParametric({ project, onClose, onCreated }: { project: Project; onClose: () => void; onCreated: (id: string) => void }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const me = useMe()
  const inp = useMemo(() => defaultInputs(project, s), [project, s])
  const src = useMemo(() => inputSources(s, project), [project, s])
  const preview = useMemo(() => buildParametric(s, project, inp, me.id), [s, project, inp, me.id])
  const t = estimateTotals(preview)
  const linear = project.asset.type === 'transmission-pipeline' || project.asset.type === 'distribution-main'
  const valve = project.asset.type === 'valve-site'
  const station = !linear && !valve
  const tax = salesTaxFor(s, project)
  const rf = s.settings.estimating.regionFactors[inp.region] ?? 1
  const regionalRows = preview.lines.filter((l) => s.rates.find((r) => r.id === l.rateId)?.region).length
  const qt = (id?: string) => s.settings.estimating.quantityTypes.find((x) => x.id === id)
  const create = () => {
    const e = buildParametric(getState(), project, inp, me.id)
    mutate((d) => { d.estimates.push(e) }, { entity: 'estimate', entityId: e.id, projectId: project.id, action: 'create', summary: `Created Class 5 rate-table draft v${e.version} from the scope statement (${describeInputs(project, inp)}; rate table v${e.rateTableVersion}): ${money(t.total)}` })
    toast(`Class 5 draft v${e.version} created`)
    onCreated(e.id)
  }
  const fact = (label: string, value: React.ReactNode, from?: string) => (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3 border-b border-line/70 py-1.5 last:border-0">
      <span className="text-muted">{label}</span>
      <span className="text-right"><b className="tabular-nums">{value}</b>{from && <span className="block text-[11px] text-faint">{from}</span>}</span>
    </div>
  )
  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} wide title="New Class 5 rate-table estimate"
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" onClick={create} disabled={!preview.lines.length}>Create draft v{preview.version}</Button></>}>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-[280px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-3">
          <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">Prices the scope from rate table <b className="text-ink">v{s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion}</b> (P50) for <b className="text-ink">{project.asset.type.replace(/-/g, ' ')}</b> · <b className="text-ink">{project.workMethod.replace(/-/g, ' ')}</b>. Inputs are read from the project's scope statement — nothing to type.</p>
          <div className="rounded border border-line px-3 py-1 text-[13px]">
            {linear && fact('Length', `${inp.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft`, src.lengthFt)}
            {(linear || valve) && fact('Diameter', `${inp.diameterIn}"`, src.diameterIn)}
            {valve && fact('Valve sites', inp.sites, src.sites)}
            {inp.tieIns != null && fact('Tie-ins', inp.tieIns, src.tieIns)}
            {inp.services != null && fact('Services', inp.services, src.services)}
            {inp.hp != null && fact('Station capacity', `${inp.hp.toLocaleString('en-US')} hp`, src.hp)}
            {fact('Region', `${inp.region} (×${rf.toFixed(2)})`, regionalRows ? `${regionalRows} regional rate row${regionalRows > 1 ? 's' : ''}; others × factor` : 'Base rates × region factor')}
            {fact('Sales tax', `${tax.pct}%`, tax.counties.map((c) => `${c.county} ${Math.round(c.share * 100)}%`).join(', '))}
          </div>
          {project.scope.quantities.length > 0 && (
            <div className="text-[12px]">
              <div className="eyebrow mb-1">Scope statement quantities</div>
              <ul className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                {project.scope.quantities.map((q, i) => <li key={i} className="flex justify-between gap-2"><span className="min-w-0 truncate">{q.label}</span><span className="shrink-0 tabular-nums text-muted">{q.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} {q.unit}{qt(q.typeId)?.feeds ? <span className="ml-1 text-faint">→ {qt(q.typeId)!.feeds}</span> : ''}</span></li>)}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
          <p className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">To change a quantity, edit the scope statement on the <a className="font-semibold text-link" href={projectHref(project.id, 'overview')}>Overview</a>; terrain-specific rows are matched from the project record ({project.location.terrain}).</p>
          {station && <Callout tone="info">Stations price as lump sums from {s.rates.filter((r) => r.assetType === project.asset.type && r.workMethod === project.workMethod)[0]?.sampleSize ?? 3} comparable stations.</Callout>}
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
            <div><div className="eyebrow">Preview total</div><div className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold tabular-nums">{money(t.total)}</div></div>
            <div className="text-right text-xs text-muted"><ClassBadge cls={preview.cls} /> range {money(t.low)}–{money(t.high)}<div>Escalation {preview.escalationPct}% · contingency {preview.contingency.pct}% · loaders {sum(preview.loaders.map((l) => l.pct)).toFixed(1)}% · tax {money(t.tax)}</div></div>
          </div>
          <div className="scroll-x max-h-[320px] overflow-y-auto rounded border border-line">
            <table className="tbl">
              <thead><tr><th>Category</th><th>Rate</th><th className="num">Qty</th><th>Unit</th><th className="num">Unit cost</th><th className="num">Total</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {preview.lines.map((l) => <tr key={l.id}><td className="text-[12px]">{shortCat(s, l.categoryId)}</td><td className="text-[12px]">{l.description}</td><td className="num">{l.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')}</td><td>{l.unit}</td><td className="num">{money(l.unitCost, l.unitCost < 1e4 ? 'full' : 'compact')}</td><td className="num font-semibold">{money(Math.round(l.qty * l.unitCost))}</td></tr>)}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            {!preview.lines.length && <p className="p-4 text-[13px] text-muted">No rate-table rows for this asset type and work method.</p>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── New version from a locked estimate
function CopyModal({ project, src, onClose, onCreated }: { project: Project; src: Estimate; onClose: () => void; onCreated: (id: string) => void }) {
  const me = useMe()
  const [split, setSplit] = useState(src.method === 'parametric' || src.method === 'quick')
  const [wps, setWps] = useState(defaultPackages(project).join(', '))
  const create = () => {
    const list = wps.split(',').map((x) => x.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    const e = buildFromExisting(getState(), src, me.id, { split: split ? list : undefined, toClass3: split })
    mutate((d) => { d.estimates.push(e) }, { entity: 'estimate', entityId: e.id, projectId: project.id, action: 'create', summary: `Created draft v${e.version} from v${src.version}${split ? ` split into ${list.join(', ')}` : ''}` })
    toast(`Draft v${e.version} created from v${src.version}`)
    onCreated(e.id)
  }
  const hasDraft = useStore((s) => s.estimates.some((e) => e.projectId === project.id && e.status === 'draft'))
  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} title={`New version from v${src.version}`} footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" onClick={create} disabled={hasDraft}>Create draft</Button></>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-[13px]">
        <p className="text-muted">Copies every line, markup, maturity item and BOE section into a new draft. v{src.version} stays locked as it was {src.status}.</p>
        {hasDraft && <Callout tone="warn">This project already has an open draft. Submit or finish it first.</Callout>}
        <label className="flex items-start gap-2"><input type="checkbox" className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4" checked={split} onChange={(e) => setSplit(e.target.checked)} /><span>Split into work packages as a Class 3 line-item estimate</span></label>
        {split && <Field label="Work packages" hint="Comma-separated. Linear quantities split evenly; lump sums go to the first package."><Input id="copy-wps" value={wps} onChange={(e) => setWps(e.target.value)} /></Field>}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Budgets (ES-10)
function BudgetsPanel({ s, project, onOpenEstimate }: { s: AppState; project: Project; onOpenEstimate: (id: string) => void }) {
  const bs = projectBudgets(s, project.id)
  const active = activeBudget(s, project.id)
  const years = Array.from(new Set(bs.flatMap((b) => Object.keys(b.byYear)))).sort()
  const cats = Array.from(new Set(bs.flatMap((b) => Object.keys(b.byCategory)))).sort((a, b) => catCode(s, a).localeCompare(catCode(s, b)))
  const [view, setView] = useState<'year' | 'cat'>('year')
  const avail = budgetKindAvailable(s, project)
  const approvedEst = s.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === project.id && e.status === 'approved').pop()
  const canCreate = !!avail && !!approvedEst && !bs.some((b) => b.estimateId === approvedEst.id && b.kind === avail.kind)
  const authFor = (bid: string) => s.workflows.filter((w) => w.recordType === 'budget' && w.recordId === bid).pop()
  const createBudget = () => {
    if (!avail || !approvedEst) return
    const t = estimateTotals(approvedEst)
    let msg = ''
    mutate((d) => {
      const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === approvedEst.id)!
      const b = createBudgetFromEstimate(d, e, avail.kind)
      b.postedToErp = false
      const inst = startWorkflow(d, 'budget-authorization', { title: `${project.code} ${avail.kind} budget from estimate v${e.version}`, recordType: 'budget', recordId: b.id, projectId: project.id, data: { amount: b.total, wbs: project.erpWbs } })
      const who = inst?.active[0]?.assigneeIds.map((id) => personName(d, id)).join(', ')
      msg = inst ? `Budget version created — authorization with ${who || 'approvers'}` : 'Budget version created'
    }, { entity: 'budget', entityId: approvedEst.id, projectId: project.id, action: 'create', summary: `Created ${avail.kind} budget version ${money(t.total)} from approved estimate v${approvedEst.version} (Class ${approvedEst.cls}); routed for budget authorization` })
    toast(msg)
  }
  const togglePost = (id: string) => {
    const b = bs.find((x) => x.id === id)!
    const wf = authFor(id)
    if (!b.postedToErp && wf && wf.status === 'running') { toast('Budget authorization is still in progress', 'info'); return }
    mutate((d) => { const x = d.budgets.find((y) => y.id === id); if (x) x.postedToErp = !x.postedToErp }, { entity: 'budget', entityId: id, projectId: project.id, action: b.postedToErp ? 'unpost' : 'post', summary: `${b.postedToErp ? 'Flagged not posted' : 'Posted'} ${b.kind} budget ${money(b.total)} to ERP WBS ${project.erpWbs}` })
    toast(b.postedToErp ? 'Marked as not posted to ERP' : `Posted to ERP WBS ${project.erpWbs}`)
  }
  const createBtn = canCreate && <Button size="sm" variant="primary" onClick={createBudget}>Create {avail!.kind} budget from v{approvedEst!.version}</Button>
  if (!bs.length) return (
    <Panel title="Budget versions" actions={createBtn}><Empty title="No budget yet">The planning budget is created from the approved Class 5 estimate at {templateFor(s, project).stages[0].gate.name}; the control budget from the approved Class 3 at the authorization gate.</Empty></Panel>
  )
  const pos = position(s, project.id)
  return (
    <Panel pad={false} title="Budget versions" subtitle={`Created from approved estimates at gate decisions · ERP WBS ${project.erpWbs}`}
      actions={<>{createBtn}<div className="inline-flex rounded border border-line bg-sunken p-0.5 text-xs">{(['year', 'cat'] as const).map((v) => <button key={v} onClick={() => setView(v)} className={cx('rounded-sm px-2.5 py-1 font-semibold', view === v ? 'bg-surface text-ink shadow-sm' : 'text-muted')}>{v === 'year' ? 'By year' : 'By category'}</button>)}</div></>}>
      <div className="scroll-x">
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Budget</th><th>From estimate</th><th>Created</th><th>ERP</th>
              <th className="num">Base</th><th className="num">Contingency</th><th className="num">Total</th>
              {view === 'year' ? years.map((y) => <th key={y} className="num">{y}</th>) : cats.map((c) => <th key={c} className="num" title={catName(s, c)}>{shortCat(s, c)}</th>)}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {bs.map((b) => {
              const e = s.estimates.find((x) => x.id === b.estimateId)
              return (
                <tr key={b.id} className={cx(b.id === active?.id && 'bg-marker/5')}>
                  <td><div className="flex items-center gap-1.5"><Badge tone={b.kind === 'control' ? 'ink' : 'neutral'}>{b.kind === 'control' ? 'Control' : 'Planning'}</Badge>{b.id === active?.id && <Badge tone="good">In force</Badge>}</div></td>
                  <td>{e ? <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-link hover:underline" onClick={() => onOpenEstimate(e.id)}><span className="font-mono">v{e.version}</span><ClassBadge cls={e.cls} /></button> : '—'}</td>
                  <td className="text-[12px]">{fmtDate(b.createdAt)}</td>
                  <td><button onClick={() => togglePost(b.id)} title="Toggle ERP posted flag">{b.postedToErp ? <Badge tone="good">Posted</Badge> : authFor(b.id)?.status === 'approved' ? <Badge tone="marker">Authorized · post to ERP</Badge> : <Badge tone="warn">Not posted</Badge>}</button>{authFor(b.id) && <div className="mt-0.5"><StatusBadge status={authFor(b.id)!.status} label={authFor(b.id)!.status === 'running' ? 'Authorizing' : undefined} /></div>}</td>
                  <td className="num">{money(b.total - b.contingency)}</td><td className="num">{money(b.contingency)}</td><td className="num font-semibold">{money(b.total)}</td>
                  {view === 'year' ? years.map((y) => <td key={y} className="num text-[12px]">{b.byYear[y] ? money(b.byYear[y]) : '—'}</td>) : cats.map((c) => <td key={c} className="num text-[12px]">{b.byCategory[c] ? money(b.byCategory[c]) : '—'}</td>)}
                </tr>
              )
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      {bs.map((b) => authFor(b.id)).filter((w) => w && w.status === 'running').map((w) => <div key={w!.id} className="border-t border-line px-4 py-2"><WorkflowStatus instanceId={w!.id} compact /></div>)}
      {active && <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-1 border-t border-line px-4 py-2 text-xs text-muted">
        <span>Budget in force <b className="text-ink">{money(pos.currentBudget)}</b>{pos.currentBudget !== active.total && <> (incl. {moneyDelta(pos.currentBudget - active.total)} approved new funding)</>}</span>
        <span>Contingency used <b className="text-ink">{money(pos.contingencyUsed)}</b> of {money(pos.contingencyOriginal)}</span>
        <span>EAC <b className={pos.eac > pos.currentBudget ? 'text-bad' : 'text-ink'}>{money(pos.eac)}</b></span>
      </div>}
    </Panel>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Compare (ES-11)
function ComparePanel({ s, ests }: { s: AppState; ests: Estimate[] }) {
  const defA = ests.length >= 2 ? ests[ests.length - 2].id : ests[0].id
  const [a, setA] = useState(defA)
  const [b, setB] = useState(ests[ests.length - 1].id)
  useEffect(() => { if (!ests.some((e) => e.id === a)) setA(defA); if (!ests.some((e) => e.id === b)) setB(ests[ests.length - 1].id) }, [ests]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const ea = ests.find((e) => e.id === a) ?? ests[0], eb = ests.find((e) => e.id === b) ?? ests[ests.length - 1]
  const br = estimateBridge(ea, eb)
  const ta = estimateTotals(ea), tb = estimateTotals(eb)
  const steps = br.steps.slice().sort((x, y) => Math.abs(y.delta) - Math.abs(x.delta))
  const top = steps.slice(0, 9)
  const rest = steps.slice(9)
  const wfSteps = [...top.map((x) => ({ label: shortCat(s, x.categoryId), delta: x.delta })), ...(rest.length ? [{ label: `Other (${rest.length})`, delta: sum(rest.map((x) => x.delta)) }] : [])]
  const cats = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(ta.byCategory), ...Object.keys(tb.byCategory)])).sort((x, y) => catCode(s, x).localeCompare(catCode(s, y)))
  const opt = ests.map((e) => ({ value: e.id, label: `v${e.version} · Class ${e.cls} · ${e.status} · ${money(estimateTotals(e).total)}` }))
  const lineCount = (e: Estimate, c: string) => e.lines.filter((l) => l.categoryId === c).length
  return (
    <Panel title="Compare versions" subtitle="Bridge from one estimate to another by cost category"
      actions={<div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs"><Select id="cmp-a" aria-label="From version" className="h-7 w-auto text-xs" value={ea.id} onChange={(e) => setA(e.target.value)} options={opt} /><span className="text-muted">to</span><Select id="cmp-b" aria-label="To version" className="h-7 w-auto text-xs" value={eb.id} onChange={(e) => setB(e.target.value)} options={opt} /></div>}>
      {ea.id === eb.id ? <Callout tone="info">Pick two different versions.</Callout> : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.25fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="mb-1 flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 text-[13px]"><span>v{ea.version} <ClassBadge cls={ea.cls} /> {money(ta.total)}</span><span className="text-muted">→</span><span>v{eb.version} <ClassBadge cls={eb.cls} /> {money(tb.total)}</span><b className={tb.total > ta.total ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good'}>{moneyDelta(tb.total - ta.total)} ({ta.total ? (tb.total >= ta.total ? '+' : '') + pct(tb.total / ta.total - 1, 1) : '—'})</b></div>
            <Waterfall start={ta.total} end={tb.total} steps={wfSteps} startLabel={`v${ea.version} C${ea.cls}`} endLabel={`v${eb.version} C${eb.cls}`} height={260} />
            <Legend items={[{ swatch: <Swatch className="bg-bad/80" />, label: 'Increase' }, { swatch: <Swatch className="bg-good/80" />, label: 'Decrease' }, { swatch: <Swatch className="bg-ink/85" />, label: 'Version total' }]} />
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 text-xs text-muted">
              <span>Range v{ea.version}: {money(ta.low)}–{money(ta.high)}</span><span>Range v{eb.version}: {money(tb.low)}–{money(tb.high)}</span>
              <span>{tb.total >= ta.low && tb.total <= ta.high ? `v${eb.version} falls inside v${ea.version}'s expected range` : `v${eb.version} falls outside v${ea.version}'s expected range`}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="scroll-x max-h-[380px] overflow-y-auto rounded border border-line">
            <table className="tbl">
              <thead><tr><th>Category</th><th className="num">v{ea.version}</th><th className="num">v{eb.version}</th><th className="num">Change</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {cats.map((c) => {
                  const x = ta.byCategory[c] || 0, y = tb.byCategory[c] || 0
                  return <tr key={c}><td className="text-[12px]"><span className="font-mono text-faint">{catCode(s, c)}</span> {catName(s, c)}<div className="text-[11px] text-faint">{lineCount(ea, c)} → {lineCount(eb, c)} lines</div></td><td className="num">{money(x)}</td><td className="num">{money(y)}</td><td className={cx('num font-semibold', y > x ? 'text-bad' : y < x ? 'text-good' : 'text-faint')}>{y === x ? '—' : moneyDelta(y - x)}</td></tr>
                })}
                <tr><td className="font-semibold">Base</td><td className="num">{money(ta.base)}</td><td className="num">{money(tb.base)}</td><td className="num font-semibold">{moneyDelta(tb.base - ta.base)}</td></tr>
                <tr><td className="text-[12px]"><span className="font-mono text-faint">{catCode(s, CONT_CAT)}</span> Contingency <span className="text-faint">({ea.contingency.pct}% → {eb.contingency.pct}%)</span></td><td className="num">{money(ta.contingency)}</td><td className="num">{money(tb.contingency)}</td><td className={cx('num font-semibold', tb.contingency > ta.contingency ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good')}>{moneyDelta(tb.contingency - ta.contingency)}</td></tr>
                <tr><td className="font-display font-bold">Total</td><td className="num font-bold">{money(ta.total)}</td><td className="num font-bold">{money(tb.total)}</td><td className="num font-bold">{moneyDelta(tb.total - ta.total)}</td></tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Panel>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Accuracy (ES-13)
function AccuracyPanel({ s, project }: { s: AppState; project: Project }) {
  const o = outcomeFor(s, project)
  const pts = accuracyForProject(s, project)
  if (!o || !pts.length) return (
    <Panel title="Estimate accuracy"><p className="text-[13px] text-muted">Accuracy is tracked once the project has actual cost. Each approved class estimate will be compared with the current forecast, then with final cost at closeout.</p></Panel>
  )
  const scaleMax = Math.max(o.value, ...pts.map((x) => x.high)) * 1.05
  return (
    <Panel title="Estimate accuracy" subtitle={o.basis === 'final' ? `Approved estimates vs final cost ${money(o.value)}` : `Approved estimates vs current forecast (EAC) ${money(o.value)} · final accuracy is set at closeout`}>
      <div className="scroll-x">
        <table className="tbl">
          <thead><tr><th>Estimate</th><th>Approved</th><th className="num">Estimate</th><th style={{ minWidth: 220 }}>AACE range · <span className="normal-case">◆ outcome</span></th><th className="num">{o.basis === 'final' ? 'Final' : 'EAC'}</th><th className="num">Accuracy</th><th>Within class range</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {pts.map((x) => (
              <tr key={x.estimateId}>
                <td><span className="font-mono">v{x.version}</span> <ClassBadge cls={x.cls} /></td>
                <td className="text-[12px]">{fmtDate(x.approvedAt?.slice(0, 10))}</td>
                <td className="num">{money(x.estimate)}</td>
                <td><OutcomeBar low={x.low} point={x.estimate} high={x.high} outcome={x.outcome} scaleMax={scaleMax} /></td>
                <td className="num">{money(x.outcome)}</td>
                <td className="num"><AccPct v={x.acc} cls={x.cls} /></td>
                <td>{x.within ? <Badge tone="good">Yes · {pct(CLASS_RANGES[x.cls].low)} / +{pct(CLASS_RANGES[x.cls].high)}</Badge> : <Badge tone="bad">No · {pct(CLASS_RANGES[x.cls].low)} / +{pct(CLASS_RANGES[x.cls].high)}</Badge>}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted">Accuracy = outcome ÷ estimate − 1. Portfolio accuracy by class, program and estimator is on <a className="font-semibold text-link" href="#estimating">Estimating</a>.</div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/Evidence.tsx =====
// Reasonableness-review package (RC-01): assembled live from the records, with a completeness score
// and the gaps a reviewer would find. Downloads as a numbered HTML binder and a CSV extract.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { mutate, useStore, personName, categoryName } from '@/core/store'
import { estimateTotals } from '@/core/engine/estimates'
import { saveFile, toCSV } from '@/core/download'
import { go, projectHref } from '@/core/router'
import { fmtDate, fmtDateTime, money, moneyDelta, sum, cx } from '@/core/util'
import { Avatar, Badge, Button, Callout, ClassBadge, KV, Panel, Progress, StatusBadge, Table, Mono, Section } from '@/ui/kit'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'
import { Icon } from '@/ui/icons'
import { binderData, evidenceRows, projectBinderHtml } from './controls/binder'
import { approvalChain, setFocusChange, setFocusSnapshot, SNAP_META, snapshotAuthority, stageLabel, type Check } from './controls/trace'

export default function Evidence({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const d = useMemo(() => binderData(s, projectId), [s, projectId])
  const { p, prog, pos, assess } = d
  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false)
  const lastExport = [...s.audit].reverse().find((a) => a.entity === 'evidence' && a.entityId === projectId)

  const dl = async (kind: 'html' | 'csv') => {
    const r = kind === 'html' ? await saveFile(`${p.code}-reasonableness-package-${s.settings.asOf}.html`, projectBinderHtml(s, projectId), 'text/html') : await saveFile(`${p.code}-evidence-extract-${s.settings.asOf}.csv`, toCSV(evidenceRows(s, projectId)), 'text/csv')
    if (r === 'saved') {
      mutate(() => {}, { entity: 'evidence', entityId: projectId, projectId, action: 'export', summary: `Downloaded ${kind === 'html' ? 'reasonableness binder' : 'evidence CSV'} (${assess.score}% complete, ${assess.gaps.length} gaps)` })
      toast(kind === 'html' ? 'Binder saved' : 'CSV extract saved')
    } else if (r === 'unavailable') toast('Download is not available here', 'bad')
  }

  const byArea = Object.entries(assess.checks.reduce<Record<string, Check[]>>((a, c) => { (a[c.area] ||= []).push(c); return a }, {}))
  const drawnChanges = d.changes.filter((c) => c.status === 'approved' && c.fundedFrom === 'contingency')
  const realized = d.risks.filter((r) => r.status === 'realized')
  const tone = assess.score >= 90 ? 'good' : assess.score >= 75 ? 'warn' : 'bad'
  let n = 0
  const H = (t: string) => `${++n}. ${t}`

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <Panel title="Reasonableness review package" subtitle={`Assembled live from ${p.code}'s records as of ${fmtDate(s.settings.asOf)}. Nothing here is typed in — every line links to its source.`}
        actions={<><Button onClick={() => dl('csv')} icon={<Icon name="download" className="h-4 w-4" />}>CSV extract</Button><Button variant="primary" onClick={() => dl('html')} icon={<Icon name="download" className="h-4 w-4" />}>Download binder</Button></>}>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
            <div className="eyebrow">Completeness</div>
            <div className={cx('font-display text-[44px] font-bold leading-none tabular-nums', tone === 'good' ? 'text-good' : tone === 'warn' ? 'text-warn' : 'text-bad')}>{assess.score}%</div>
            <Progress value={assess.score} tone={tone} />
            <div className="text-xs text-muted">{assess.passed} of {assess.checks.length} checks pass · {assess.gaps.filter((g) => g.severity === 'major').length} major gaps · {assess.gaps.filter((g) => g.severity === 'minor').length} minor</div>
            {lastExport && <div className="text-xs text-faint">Last downloaded {fmtDateTime(lastExport.at)} by {personName(s, lastExport.actorId)}</div>}
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1">
              {byArea.map(([area, cs]) => { const ok = cs.filter((c) => c.ok).length; return <div key={area} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-[12px]"><span>{area}</span><span className={cx('tabular-nums', ok === cs.length ? 'text-good' : 'text-warn')}>{ok}/{cs.length}</span></div> })}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between"><h3 className="eyebrow">{assess.gaps.length ? `Gaps a reviewer would find (${assess.gaps.length})` : 'No gaps found'}</h3><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setShowAll(!showAll)}>{showAll ? 'Show gaps only' : `Show all ${assess.checks.length} checks`}</Button></div>
            {assess.gaps.length === 0 && !showAll && <Callout tone="good">Every change, baseline, estimate and variance on {p.code} traces to an approved decision with its evidence.</Callout>}
            <ul className="flex max-h-[300px] flex-col divide-y divide-line overflow-y-auto rounded-md border border-line">
              {(showAll ? assess.checks : assess.gaps).map((c) => (
                <li key={c.id} className="flex items-start gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px]">
                  <span className={cx('mt-0.5 shrink-0', c.ok ? 'text-good' : c.severity === 'major' ? 'text-bad' : 'text-warn')}><Icon name={c.ok ? 'check' : 'alert'} className="h-4 w-4" /></span>
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{c.ok ? c.label : c.gap}<span className="ml-1.5 text-xs text-faint">{c.area}{!c.ok && ` · ${c.severity}`}</span></span>
                  {!c.ok && c.tab && <a href={projectHref(projectId, c.tab)} className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold text-link hover:underline">Fix</a>}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Panel>

      <Panel title={H('Cover')}>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[3fr_2fr]">
          <KV cols={2} items={[
            ['Project', <span><Mono className="font-semibold">{p.code}</Mono> {p.name}</span>], ['Program', `${prog.code} — ${prog.name}`],
            ['Regulatory driver', p.regulatoryCitation], ['Proceeding', p.fundingProceeding],
            ['Recovery mechanism', prog.recovery], ['Stage / status', `${stageLabel(s, p, p.stageIndex)} · ${p.status}`],
            ['Sponsor', personName(s, p.team.sponsor)], ['Project manager', personName(s, p.team.pm)],
          ]} />
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-line p-3 text-[13px]">
            <Row k="Original authorized" v={money(pos.originalBudget, 'full')} />
            <Row k="Approved changes" v={moneyDelta(pos.approvedChanges)} />
            <Row k="Current authorized" v={money(pos.currentBudget, 'full')} strong />
            <Row k="Recorded to date (ERP)" v={money(pos.actualToDate, 'full')} />
            <Row k="Forecast at completion" v={money(pos.eac, 'full')} strong />
            <Row k="Recorded vs authorized" v={pos.currentBudget ? `${((pos.actualToDate / pos.currentBudget) * 100).toFixed(1)}%` : '—'} />
            <Row k="Forecast vs authorized" v={moneyDelta(pos.eac - pos.currentBudget)} tone={pos.eac > pos.currentBudget ? 'bad' : 'good'} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Panel>

      <Panel title={H('Estimate history')} subtitle="Each estimate version, its AACE class, and who approved it" pad={false}>
        <Table rows={d.ests} rowKey={(e) => e.id} empty={<p className="p-4 text-[13px] text-muted">No estimates yet.</p>} columns={[
          { key: 'v', header: 'Version', render: (e) => <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Mono>v{e.version}</Mono><ClassBadge cls={e.cls} /></span> },
          { key: 't', header: 'Title', render: (e) => <span className="block min-w-[180px]">{e.title}<span className="block text-xs text-muted">{e.method} · {e.lines.length} lines · contingency {e.contingency.pct}% {e.contingency.method}</span></span> },
          { key: 'tot', header: 'Total', num: true, render: (e) => money(estimateTotals(e).total) },
          { key: 'st', header: 'Status', render: (e) => <StatusBadge status={e.status} /> },
          { key: 'ap', header: 'Approved by', render: (e) => (e.approvedById ? <span className="text-[12px]">{personName(s, e.approvedById)}<span className="block text-faint">{fmtDateTime(e.approvedAt)}</span></span> : <span className="text-faint">—</span>) },
          { key: 'boe', header: 'Basis of estimate', render: (e) => <span className="text-xs text-muted">{e.basis.assumptions.length} assumptions · {e.basis.exclusions.length} exclusions</span> },
          { key: 'sig', header: 'Chain', render: (e) => <span className="text-xs">{approvalChain(s, e.workflowInstanceId).filter((x) => x.sig).length} e-signatures</span> },
        ]} />
      </Panel>

      <Panel title={H('Baselines and approving decisions')} subtitle="Original, Current, gate and regulatory snapshots — monthly period closes omitted here, included in the binder count" pad={false}>
        <Table rows={d.snaps.filter((x) => x.type !== 'period-close')} rowKey={(x) => x.id} dense onRowClick={(x) => { setFocusSnapshot(x.id); go(projectHref(projectId, 'baselines')) }} empty={<p className="p-4 text-[13px] text-muted">No baselines yet.</p>} columns={[
          { key: 'd', header: 'Date', render: (x) => fmtDate(x.date), width: 110 },
          { key: 't', header: 'Type', render: (x) => <Badge tone={SNAP_META[x.type].tone === 'ink' ? 'ink' : SNAP_META[x.type].tone}>{SNAP_META[x.type].label}</Badge> },
          { key: 'l', header: 'Snapshot', render: (x) => <span className="block min-w-[180px]">{x.label}</span> },
          { key: 'b', header: 'Budget', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.budget.total) },
          { key: 'a', header: 'Approving decision', render: (x) => { const a = snapshotAuthority(s, x); return <span className="block min-w-[240px] text-[12px]"><b className={a.kind === 'Gate decision' || a.kind === 'Approved change' ? 'text-good' : 'text-ink'}>{a.kind}</b> · {a.text}{a.signers.length > 0 && <span className="block text-faint">{a.signers.map((g) => g.name + (g.sig ? ` · sig ${g.sig.slice(0, 8)}` : '')).join('; ')}</span>}</span> } },
        ]} />
      </Panel>

      <Panel title={H('Changes and approval chains')} subtitle="Every signature on every change, in order" >
        {d.changes.length ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {d.changes.map((c) => {
              const chain = approvalChain(s, c.workflowInstanceId)
              return (
                <div key={c.id} className="rounded-md border border-line">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-b border-line bg-sunken/50 px-3 py-2 text-[13px]">
                    <a href={projectHref(projectId, 'changes')} onClick={() => setFocusChange(c.id)} className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-link hover:underline">{c.code}</a>
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 font-semibold">{c.title}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted">{c.rootCause} · {categoryName(s, c.categoryId)} · {c.fundedFrom.replace('-', ' ')}</span>
                    <b className={cx('tabular-nums', c.costImpact > 0 ? 'text-bad' : 'text-good')}>{moneyDelta(c.costImpact)}</b>
                    <StatusBadge status={c.status} />
                  </div>
                  <ol className="flex flex-col px-3 py-1.5">
                    {chain.map((x, i) => (
                      <li key={i} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 py-1 text-[12.5px]">
                        {x.actorId !== 'system' ? <Avatar name={x.actor} size={18} /> : <span className="inline-block h-[18px] w-[18px]" />}
                        <b>{x.actor}</b><span className="text-muted">{x.title}</span><span className="text-muted">· {x.step} · {x.action}</span>
                        {x.sig && <Badge tone="good">e-signed <Mono className="text-[10.5px]">{x.sig}</Mono></Badge>}
                        <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-faint">{fmtDateTime(x.at)}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                    {!chain.length && <li className="py-1 text-[12.5px] text-bad">No workflow history on record.</li>}
                  </ol>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        ) : <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">No changes on record.</p>}
      </Panel>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <Panel title={H('Variance explanations')} pad={false}>
          <Table rows={d.variances} rowKey={(v) => v.id} dense empty={<p className="p-4 text-[13px] text-muted">No threshold breaches have required an explanation.</p>} columns={[
            { key: 'd', header: 'Variance', render: (v) => <span className="block min-w-[200px]">{v.description}<span className="block text-xs text-muted">{v.narrative || 'Narrative not yet written'}</span></span> },
            { key: 'r', header: 'Root cause', render: (v) => v.rootCause ?? '—' },
            { key: 'c', header: 'Changes', render: (v) => <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px]">{v.changeIds.map((id) => s.changes.find((c) => c.id === id)?.code).join(', ') || '—'}</span> },
            { key: 's', header: 'Status', render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v.status} /> },
          ]} />
        </Panel>
        <Panel title={H('Risks realized vs contingency drawn')}>
          <div className="mb-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-[13px]">
            <Fig k="Contingency at authorization" v={money(pos.contingencyOriginal)} />
            <Fig k="Drawn" v={money(pos.contingencyUsed)} sub={`${drawnChanges.length} changes · ${d.draws.filter((x) => x.status === 'approved' && !x.changeId).length} draws`} />
            <Fig k="Remaining" v={money(pos.contingencyRemaining)} tone={pos.contingencyRemaining < 0 ? 'bad' : undefined} />
          </div>
          <Table rows={[...realized.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, kind: 'Realized risk', ref: r.code, text: r.title, amt: sum(d.draws.filter((x) => x.riskId === r.id && x.status === 'approved').map((x) => x.amount)) + sum(d.changes.filter((c) => c.riskId === r.id && c.status === 'approved').map((c) => c.costImpact)), ml: r.cost.ml })), ...drawnChanges.filter((c) => !c.riskId).map((c) => ({ id: c.id, kind: 'Change (no risk link)', ref: c.code, text: c.title, amt: c.costImpact, ml: 0 }))]} rowKey={(x) => x.id} dense empty={<p className="text-[13px] text-muted">No contingency drawn.</p>} columns={[
            { key: 'k', header: 'Source', render: (x) => <span className="text-[12px]">{x.kind}<span className="block font-mono text-faint">{x.ref}</span></span> },
            { key: 't', header: 'Description', render: (x) => <span className="text-[12.5px]">{x.text}</span> },
            { key: 'ml', header: 'Risk ML', num: true, render: (x) => (x.ml ? money(x.ml) : '—') },
            { key: 'a', header: 'Drawn', num: true, render: (x) => money(x.amt) },
          ]} />
        </Panel>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-3">
        <Panel title={H('Safety record')}>
          <KV cols={1} items={[
            ['Safety plans', d.plans.length ? d.plans.map((x) => <div key={x.id}>{x.workPackage} v{x.version} <StatusBadge status={x.status} />{x.approvedAt && <span className="text-xs text-muted"> · approved {fmtDate(x.approvedAt)}</span>}</div>) : 'None required yet'],
            ['Tailboards', `${d.tailboards.length} recorded${d.tailboards.length ? `, last ${fmtDate(d.tailboards.map((t) => t.date).sort().pop())}` : ''}`],
            ['Events', `${d.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'observation').length} observations · ${d.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'near-miss').length} near misses · ${d.events.filter((e) => e.type === 'incident').length} incidents`],
            ['SIF potential', `${d.events.filter((e) => e.sifPotential).length} (${d.events.filter((e) => e.sifPotential && e.status === 'open').length} open)`],
          ]} />
        </Panel>
        <Panel title={H('Records completeness')}>
          {d.recs.length ? (() => {
            const v = d.recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'verified').length, a = d.recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'attached').length, m = d.recs.length - v - a
            return (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-[13px]">
                <div className="flex h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-sunken"><span className="bg-good" style={{ width: `${(v / d.recs.length) * 100}%` }} /><span className="bg-info/60" style={{ width: `${(a / d.recs.length) * 100}%` }} /><span className="bg-bad/70" style={{ width: `${(m / d.recs.length) * 100}%` }} /></div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 text-xs text-muted"><span><b className="text-good">{v}</b> verified</span><span><b className="text-info">{a}</b> attached</span><span><b className="text-bad">{m}</b> missing</span></div>
                <ul className="text-[12.5px] text-muted">{d.recs.filter((r) => r.status === 'missing').slice(0, 5).map((r) => <li key={r.id}>Missing: {r.type} — {r.segment}</li>)}</ul>
                <a href={projectHref(projectId, 'records')} className="text-xs font-semibold text-link hover:underline">Open records</a>
              </div>
            )
          })() : <p className="text-[13px] text-muted">Construction records are required from the construction stage.</p>}
        </Panel>
        <Panel title={H('Gate decisions')} pad={false}>
          <Table rows={d.gates} rowKey={(g) => g.id} dense empty={<p className="p-4 text-[13px] text-muted">No gates passed yet.</p>} columns={[
            { key: 'g', header: 'Gate', render: (g) => <span className="text-[12.5px]">{g.gateName}<span className="block text-faint">{fmtDate(g.date)} · {g.decidedBy.map((id) => personName(s, id)).join(', ')}</span></span> },
            { key: 'o', header: 'Outcome', render: (g) => <StatusBadge status={g.outcome} /> },
            { key: 'r', header: 'Ready', num: true, render: (g) => `${g.readiness}%` },
          ]} />
        </Panel>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function Row({ k, v, strong, tone }: { k: string; v: string; strong?: boolean; tone?: 'good' | 'bad' }) {
  return <div className="flex justify-between gap-3"><span className="text-muted">{k}</span><span className={cx('tabular-nums', strong && 'font-bold', tone === 'bad' && 'text-bad', tone === 'good' && 'text-good')}>{v}</span></div>
}
function Fig({ k, v, sub, tone }: { k: string; v: string; sub?: string; tone?: 'bad' }) {
  return <div className="min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line px-2.5 py-2"><div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">{k}</div><div className={cx('font-display text-[17px] font-bold tabular-nums', tone === 'bad' && 'text-bad')}>{v}</div>{sub && <div className="text-[11px] text-muted">{sub}</div>}</div>
}

===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/Finance.tsx =====
// Finance & commitments: the project's financial accounts (WOA + internal orders), the commitments
// ("call-offs") it buys through, and the RFI / change orders that are the single entry point for
// changing a commitment. Two coverage checks close the loop: every WBS function with budget is carried
// by an IO, and every estimate line is bought through a vendor commitment (or is company labor).
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { mutate, personName, useMe, useStore } from '@/core/store'
import type { AppState, Contract, InternalOrder, Project } from '@/core/types'
import { capitalSplit, commitmentIssues, commitmentsFor, ioCoverage, ioLabel, lineCoverage, NON_COMMITMENT_LINES, type CommitmentRow } from '@/core/engine/finance'
import { reviseInfo } from '@/core/engine/info'
import { lineById } from '@/core/engine/lines'
import { go, projectHref } from '@/core/router'
import { fmtDate, money, moneyDelta, sum, cx } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, Empty, Input, Mono, Panel, Select, SegButtons, Stat, StatusBadge, Table } from '@/ui/kit'
import { Icon } from '@/ui/icons'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'
import { lsSet } from '../../admin/common'
import { setFocusChange } from './controls/trace'
import { CcoModal, type CcoSeed } from './finance/CcoModal'
import { CommitmentDetail } from './finance/CommitmentDetail'
import { CommitmentForm } from './finance/CommitmentForm'
import { IoDrawer } from './finance/IoDrawer'
import { takeFocusCommitment } from './finance/focus'

export default function Finance({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  return <FinanceInner key={projectId} projectId={projectId} />
}

const EDIT_ROLES = ['admin', 'pmo', 'finance', 'project-controls', 'plant-accounting']

function FinanceInner({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const me = useMe()
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const canEdit = p.status !== 'closed' && p.status !== 'cancelled' && (me.id === p.team.pm || me.id === p.team.projectControls || me.roles.some((r) => EDIT_ROLES.includes(r)))
  const rows = useMemo(() => commitmentsFor(s, p.id), [s, p.id])
  const ioCov = useMemo(() => ioCoverage(s, p), [s, p])
  const lineCov = useMemo(() => lineCoverage(s, p.id), [s, p.id])
  const ios = p.finance?.ios ?? []

  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<{ id: string; cco?: string } | null>(null)
  const [cmEdit, setCmEdit] = useState<{ c?: Contract; lineIds?: string[] } | null>(null)
  const [ioEdit, setIoEdit] = useState<{ io?: InternalOrder; functionIds?: string[] } | null>(null)
  const [cco, setCco] = useState<CcoSeed | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => { const f = takeFocusCommitment(); if (f) setDetail({ id: f.contractId, cco: f.ccoId }) }, [])

  const live = rows.filter((r) => r.c.status !== 'rfp')
  const current = sum(live.map((r) => r.current))
  const invoiced = sum(live.map((r) => r.invoiced))
  const pending = s.commitmentChanges.filter((x) => x.projectId === p.id && x.status === 'in-review')
  const mine = pending.filter((x) => s.workflows.find((w) => w.id === x.workflowInstanceId)?.active.some((a) => a.assigneeIds.includes(me.id) && !a.actedIds.includes(me.id)))
  const gapCount = ioCov.gaps.length + lineCov.gaps.length
  const openIos = ios.filter((x) => x.status === 'open').length, reqIos = ios.filter((x) => x.status === 'requested').length
  const woa = p.finance?.woa || p.info?.workOrder

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-5">
        <Stat label="Work order authorization" value={woa ? <Mono className="text-[19px]">{woa}</Mono> : <span className="text-faint">Not issued</span>} sub={woa ? 'Charges authorized' : 'Issued with phase 2 funding'} />
        <Stat label="Internal orders" value={ios.length} sub={ios.length ? `${openIos} open · ${reqIos} requested` : 'None requested yet'} tone={reqIos && !openIos ? 'warn' : undefined} />
        <Stat label="Current commitments" value={money(current)} sub={`${live.length} commitment${live.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${moneyDelta(sum(live.map((r) => r.approvedChanges)))} approved changes`} />
        <Stat label="Remaining to invoice" value={money(current - invoiced)} sub={`${money(invoiced)} invoiced${pending.length ? ` · ${moneyDelta(sum(pending.map((x) => x.amount)))} in review` : ''}`} />
        <Stat label="Coverage gaps" value={gapCount} tone={gapCount ? 'warn' : 'good'} sub={`${ioCov.gaps.length} WBS function${ioCov.gaps.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${lineCov.gaps.length} estimate line${lineCov.gaps.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`} />
      </div>

      {mine.length > 0 && (
        <Callout tone="marker" title={`${mine.length} RFI / change order${mine.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} waiting on you`}>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            {mine.map((x) => <div key={x.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"><Mono className="font-semibold">{x.code}</Mono><span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{x.rfi}</span><b className="tabular-nums">{moneyDelta(x.amount)}</b><Button size="sm" onClick={() => setDetail({ id: x.contractId, cco: x.id })}>Review</Button></div>)}
          </div>
        </Callout>
      )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
        <AccountsPanel s={s} p={p} canEdit={canEdit} onEditIo={(io) => setIoEdit({ io })} onAddIo={() => setIoEdit({})} />
        <IoCoveragePanel p={p} cov={ioCov} canEdit={canEdit} onNewIo={(fn) => setIoEdit({ functionIds: [fn] })} />
      </div>

      <Panel title="Commitments" subtitle="Vendor POs this project buys through and the share of each PO intended for it. Click a commitment for its RFI / change order log." pad={false}
        actions={canEdit && <>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={() => setCco({})} disabled={!live.some((r) => r.c.status !== 'closed')}>New RFI / change order</Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="primary" icon={<Icon name="plus" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />} onClick={() => setCmEdit({})}>Add commitment</Button>
        </>}>
        <CommitmentTable s={s} p={p} rows={rows} onOpen={(id) => setDetail({ id })} />
      </Panel>

      <LineCoveragePanel cov={lineCov} canEdit={canEdit} onCover={(lineId) => setCmEdit({ lineIds: [lineId] })} onOpen={(id) => setDetail({ id })} s={s} />

      <CcoLog s={s} p={p} onOpen={(contractId, id) => setDetail({ id: contractId, cco: id })} />

      <Panel title="How a change reaches vendors, the IO and the schedule">
        <ol className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 text-[12.5px] md:grid-cols-4">
          <FlowStep n={1} title="Change request" text="A change to the project baseline (scope, cost, schedule) is raised on the Changes tab and names the commitments it affects." href={projectHref(p.id, 'changes')} />
          <FlowStep n={2} title="RFI / change order" text="Each affected commitment gets an RFI / CCO here — the only way a commitment changes. Approval adds the amount to the commitment." />
          <FlowStep n={3} title="Accounts" text="The commitment charges an internal order; the IO must carry the WBS function of every line the commitment covers." />
          <FlowStep n={4} title="Scheduler task" text="When a change moves dates, the scheduler gets a task and a notification to update P6. Crosswalk does not write to P6." />
        </ol>
      </Panel>

      {detail && <CommitmentDetail key={detail.id + (detail.cco ?? '')} contractId={detail.id} focusCco={detail.cco} canEdit={canEdit} onClose={() => setDetail(null)}
        onEdit={() => { const c = s.contracts.find((x) => x.id === detail.id); setDetail(null); if (c) setCmEdit({ c }) }}
        onNewCco={() => { setCco({ contractId: detail.id }) }} />}
      <CommitmentForm p={p} edit={cmEdit} onClose={() => setCmEdit(null)} />
      <IoDrawer p={p} edit={ioEdit} onClose={() => setIoEdit(null)} />
      <CcoModal projectId={p.id} seed={cco} onClose={() => setCco(null)} onCreated={(id, contractId) => setDetail({ id: contractId, cco: id })} />
    </div>
  )
}

function FlowStep({ n, title, text, href }: { n: number; title: string; text: string; href?: string }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex gap-2.5 rounded-md border border-line px-3 py-2">
      <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-ink text-[11px] font-bold text-surface">{n}</span>
      <span className="min-w-0"><b className="block text-[13px]">{href ? <a href={href} className="text-link hover:underline">{title}</a> : title}</b><span className="text-muted">{text}</span></span>
    </li>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Accounts: WOA, IOs, capital / O&M split
function AccountsPanel({ s, p, canEdit, onEditIo, onAddIo }: { s: AppState; p: Project; canEdit: boolean; onEditIo: (io: InternalOrder) => void; onAddIo: () => void }) {
  const woa = p.finance?.woa || p.info?.workOrder || ''
  const [val, setVal] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const split = capitalSplit(s, p)
  const ios = p.finance?.ios ?? []
  const saveWoa = () => {
    const v = (val ?? '').trim()
    if (v === woa) { setVal(null); return }
    mutate((d) => {
      const x = d.projects.find((y) => y.id === p.id)!
      x.finance ??= { ios: [] }
      x.finance.woa = v || undefined
      reviseInfo(d, p.id, { workOrder: v }, 'WOA set on the Finance tab')
    }, { entity: 'finance', entityId: p.id, projectId: p.id, action: 'woa', summary: `${p.code}: WOA ${woa || '(none)'} → ${v || '(none)'}` })
    toast(v ? `WOA ${v} saved` : 'WOA cleared')
    setVal(null)
  }
  const openSap = () => { lsSet('crosswalk:admin-tab', 'sap'); go('#admin') }
  const cap = split.scopeCapitalPct
  return (
    <Panel title="Financial accounts" subtitle="Key facts on the Overview show these; manage them here."
      actions={canEdit && <Button size="sm" icon={<Icon name="plus" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />} onClick={onAddIo}>Add IO</Button>}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-faint">Work order authorization (WOA)</div>
            {val === null ? (
              <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2">{woa ? <Mono className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{woa}</Mono> : <span className="text-[13px] text-faint">Not issued</span>}{canEdit && <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setVal(woa)}>{woa ? 'Edit' : 'Enter WOA'}</Button>}</div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2"><Input id="fin-woa" aria-label="WOA number" className="w-44 font-mono" value={val} onChange={(e) => setVal(e.target.value)} placeholder={`WOA-${p.code.slice(4)}-01`} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && saveWoa()} /><Button size="sm" variant="primary" onClick={saveWoa}>Save</Button><Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setVal(null)}>Cancel</Button></div>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-[11.5px] text-muted">Saving also revises the Project Information record (WOA field), so both always agree.</p>
        </div>

        <Table rows={ios} rowKey={(io) => io.id} dense empty={<Empty title="No internal orders yet">IOs are requested once the project is funded past initiation. Add the capital IO (and an O&M IO when the scope has an O&M share), then pick the WBS functions each one carries.</Empty>} columns={[
          { key: 'n', header: 'Internal order', render: (io) => <span><Mono className="font-semibold">{io.number}</Mono><span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted">{io.description}</span></span> },
          { key: 't', header: 'Type', render: (io) => <Badge tone={io.type === 'capital' ? 'ink' : 'info'}>{io.type === 'capital' ? 'Capital' : 'O&M'}</Badge> },
          { key: 's', header: 'Status', render: (io) => <StatusBadge status={io.status} label={io.status === 'open' ? 'Open' : undefined} /> },
          { key: 'o', header: 'Opened', render: (io) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums">{fmtDate(io.opened)}</span> },
          { key: 'f', header: 'Carries', render: (io) => <span className="flex max-w-[240px] flex-wrap gap-1">{io.functionIds.map((f) => <Mono key={f} className="rounded bg-sunken px-1 text-[11px]">{s.settings.wbsFunctions.find((w) => w.id === f)?.code ?? f}</Mono>)}</span> },
          { key: 'e', header: '', render: (io) => canEdit ? <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onEditIo(io)}>Edit</Button> : null },
        ]} />

        <div className="rounded-md border border-line p-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="eyebrow">Capital vs O&M</div>
            <a href={projectHref(p.id, 'overview')} className="text-[11.5px] font-semibold text-link hover:underline">Scope statement split →</a>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-2 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <SplitBar label="Scope statement" capital={cap} />
            <SplitBar label="Carried by the IOs" capital={split.ioCapitalPct} sub={split.capital + split.om ? `${money(split.capital)} capital · ${money(split.om)} O&M${split.unassigned ? ` · ${money(split.unassigned)} on no IO` : ''}` : 'No budget on an IO yet'} />
          </div>
          {split.deltaPts !== undefined && Math.abs(split.deltaPts) > 5 && (
            <Callout tone="warn">The IOs carry {split.ioCapitalPct}% capital but the scope statement says {cap}%. Move WBS functions between the capital and O&M IOs, or correct the split in the scope statement.</Callout>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[12px] text-muted">
          <Icon name="layers" className="h-4 w-4 text-faint" />
          <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">Cost elements are universal: SAP cost elements map once to forecast lines for every project.</span>
          <button type="button" onClick={openSap} className="font-semibold text-link hover:underline">Configuration → SAP mapping</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Panel>
  )
}

function SplitBar({ label, capital, sub }: { label: string; capital?: number; sub?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 text-[12px]"><span className="font-semibold">{label}</span><span className="tabular-nums text-muted">{capital === undefined ? '—' : `${capital}% capital · ${Math.round((100 - capital) * 10) / 10}% O&M`}</span></div>
      <div className="mt-1 flex h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-sunken">{capital !== undefined && <><span className="h-full bg-ink" style={{ width: `${capital}%` }} /><span className="h-full bg-info" style={{ width: `${100 - capital}%` }} /></>}</div>
      {sub && <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-faint">{sub}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── IO coverage by WBS function (feedback loop)
function IoCoveragePanel({ p, cov, canEdit, onNewIo }: { p: Project; cov: ReturnType<typeof ioCoverage>; canEdit: boolean; onNewIo: (fnId: string) => void }) {
  const [all, setAll] = useState(false)
  const ios = (p.finance?.ios ?? []).filter((x) => x.status !== 'closed')
  const rows = cov.rows.filter((r) => all || r.budget > 0)
  const assign = (fnId: string, ioId: string) => {
    if (ioId === '__new') { onNewIo(fnId); return }
    const io = ios.find((x) => x.id === ioId)
    if (!io) return
    const fn = cov.rows.find((r) => r.fnId === fnId)
    mutate((d) => { const x = d.projects.find((y) => y.id === p.id)!.finance!.ios.find((y) => y.id === ioId)!; if (!x.functionIds.includes(fnId)) x.functionIds.push(fnId) },
      { entity: 'internal-order', entityId: ioId, projectId: p.id, action: 'update', summary: `${p.code}: IO ${io.number} now carries ${fn?.code} ${fn?.name}` })
    toast(`${fn?.code} assigned to IO ${io.number}`)
  }
  const tone = (st: string) => (st === 'ok' ? 'good' : st === 'pending' ? 'info' : st === 'duplicate' ? 'warn' : 'bad') as 'good' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'bad'
  const label = (st: string) => ({ ok: 'Carried', pending: 'IO requested', gap: 'No IO', duplicate: 'Two IOs', closed: 'IO closed' } as Record<string, string>)[st]
  return (
    <Panel title="IO coverage by WBS function" subtitle={`Every function with budget must be carried by one live internal order · amounts from the ${cov.basisLabel}`} pad={false}
      actions={<SegButtons options={[{ id: 'budget', label: 'With budget' }, { id: 'all', label: 'All' }]} value={all ? 'all' : 'budget'} onChange={(v) => setAll(v === 'all')} />}>
      {cov.basis === 'none' ? <div className="p-4"><Empty title="No estimate yet">Coverage is checked once the project has an estimate or budget.</Empty></div> : (
        <>
          {cov.gaps.length > 0 ? <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-2 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-warn">{cov.gaps.length} function{cov.gaps.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} with budget not carried by an open IO — {money(sum(cov.gaps.map((g) => g.budget)))}</div>
            : <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-line px-4 py-2 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-good"><Icon name="check" className="h-4 w-4" />Every function with budget is carried by an open IO</div>}
          <Table rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.fnId} dense columns={[
            { key: 'f', header: 'WBS function', render: (r) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap"><Mono className="text-faint">{r.code}</Mono> {r.name}</span> },
            { key: 'b', header: 'Budget', num: true, render: (r) => (r.budget ? money(r.budget) : <span className="text-faint">—</span>), sort: (r) => r.budget },
            { key: 'io', header: 'IO', render: (r) => r.ios.length ? <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">{r.ios.map((io) => <Mono key={io.id} className={cx('rounded px-1 text-[11px]', io.status === 'closed' ? 'bg-sunken text-faint line-through' : 'bg-sunken')}>{io.number}</Mono>)}</span> : <span className="text-faint">—</span> },
            { key: 's', header: 'Check', render: (r) => r.budget > 0 || r.ios.length ? <Badge tone={tone(r.status)} title={r.note}>{label(r.status)}</Badge> : <span className="text-[11.5px] text-faint">No budget</span> },
            { key: 'a', header: '', render: (r) => canEdit && r.budget > 0 && (r.status === 'gap' || r.status === 'closed') ? (
              <Select aria-label={`Assign ${r.code} to an IO`} className="h-7 w-36 py-0 text-[12px]" value="" onChange={(e) => assign(r.fnId, e.target.value)}
                options={[{ value: '', label: 'Assign to IO…' }, ...ios.map((io) => ({ value: io.id, label: ioLabel(io) })), { value: '__new', label: 'New IO…' }]} />
            ) : null },
          ]} />
        </>
      )}
    </Panel>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Commitments table
function CommitmentTable({ s, p, rows, onOpen }: { s: AppState; p: Project; rows: CommitmentRow[]; onOpen: (id: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <Table rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.c.id} onRowClick={(r) => onOpen(r.c.id)} empty={<div className="p-4"><Empty title="No commitments yet">Add each vendor PO the project will use, with the share of the PO intended for this project and the forecast lines it buys.</Empty></div>} columns={[
      { key: 'v', header: 'Vendor', sort: (r) => r.c.vendor, render: (r) => <span className="block min-w-[180px]"><span className="font-semibold">{r.c.vendor || 'TBD'}</span> <span className="text-[11px] text-faint">{r.c.type}</span><span className="block max-w-[260px] truncate text-[11.5px] text-muted" title={r.c.scope}>{r.c.scope || 'No scope stated'}</span></span> },
      { key: 'po', header: 'PO', render: (r) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap"><Mono>{r.c.poNumber || '—'}</Mono>{r.c.status === 'rfp' && <Badge className="ml-1">RFP</Badge>}</span> },
      { key: 'pt', header: 'PO total', num: true, sort: (r) => r.c.poTotal ?? 0, render: (r) => money(r.c.poTotal) },
      { key: 'pct', header: '% of PO', num: true, sort: (r) => r.c.pctOfPo ?? 0, render: (r) => <span title={r.usedPct !== undefined ? `Current commitment uses ${r.usedPct}% of the PO` : undefined} className={cx(r.overShare && 'font-semibold text-warn')}>{r.c.pctOfPo !== undefined ? `${r.c.pctOfPo}%` : '—'}{r.overShare && <span className="block text-[10.5px]">uses {r.usedPct}%</span>}</span> },
      { key: 'cm', header: 'Committed', num: true, sort: (r) => r.committed, render: (r) => money(r.committed) },
      { key: 'ch', header: 'Approved changes', num: true, sort: (r) => r.approvedChanges, render: (r) => <span>{r.approvedChanges ? moneyDelta(r.approvedChanges) : '—'}{r.pendingChanges !== 0 && <span className="block text-[10.5px] text-info">{moneyDelta(r.pendingChanges)} in review</span>}</span> },
      { key: 'cur', header: 'Current', num: true, sort: (r) => r.current, render: (r) => <b>{money(r.current)}</b> },
      { key: 'inv', header: 'Invoiced', num: true, sort: (r) => r.invoiced, render: (r) => money(r.invoiced) },
      { key: 'rem', header: 'Remaining', num: true, sort: (r) => r.remaining, render: (r) => <span className={cx(r.remaining < 0 && 'font-semibold text-bad')}>{money(r.remaining)}</span> },
      { key: 'l', header: 'Lines', render: (r) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[12px]" title={(r.c.lineIds ?? []).map((l) => `${lineById(s, l)?.code} ${lineById(s, l)?.name}`).join('\n')}>{(r.c.lineIds ?? []).map((l) => lineById(s, l)?.code).join(', ') || <span className="text-bad">none</span>}</span> },
      { key: 'io', header: 'IO', render: (r) => { const io = p.finance?.ios.find((x) => x.id === r.c.ioId); return io ? <Mono className="text-[11.5px]">{io.number}</Mono> : <span className="text-[12px] text-bad">none</span> } },
      { key: 'x', header: 'Checks', render: (r) => { const iss = commitmentIssues(s, p, r.c); return iss.length ? <Badge tone="warn" title={iss.join('\n')}>{iss.length} issue{iss.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}</Badge> : <Badge tone="good">OK</Badge> } },
    ]} />
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Estimate line coverage
function LineCoveragePanel({ s, cov, canEdit, onCover, onOpen }: { s: AppState; cov: ReturnType<typeof lineCoverage>; canEdit: boolean; onCover: (lineId: string) => void; onOpen: (contractId: string) => void }) {
  const [only, setOnly] = useState(cov.gaps.length > 0)
  const rows = cov.rows.filter((r) => !only || r.status === 'gap')
  return (
    <Panel title="Estimate line coverage" subtitle={`Every estimate line must be assigned to a vendor commitment · amounts from the ${cov.basisLabel}`} pad={false}
      actions={<SegButtons options={[{ id: 'gaps', label: `Uncovered ${cov.gaps.length}` }, { id: 'all', label: `All ${cov.rows.length}` }]} value={only ? 'gaps' : 'all'} onChange={(v) => setOnly(v === 'gaps')} />}>
      {cov.basis === 'none' ? <div className="p-4"><Empty title="No estimate yet">Line coverage is checked once the project has an estimate.</Empty></div> : (
        <>
          {cov.gaps.length > 0 ? <div className="border-b border-line px-4 py-2 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-warn">{cov.gaps.length} line{cov.gaps.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} ({money(cov.uncovered)}) not assigned to any commitment — add the PO that buys each one.</div>
            : <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-line px-4 py-2 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-good"><Icon name="check" className="h-4 w-4" />Every estimate line is covered by a commitment or company labor</div>}
          <Table rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.lineId} dense empty={<div className="p-4 text-[13px] text-muted">No uncovered lines.</div>} columns={[
            { key: 'l', header: 'Forecast line', render: (r) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap"><Mono className="text-faint">{r.code}</Mono> {r.name}</span> },
            { key: 'b', header: 'Budget', num: true, sort: (r) => r.budget, render: (r) => money(r.budget) },
            { key: 'c', header: 'Committed (attributed)', num: true, sort: (r) => r.committed, render: (r) => (r.contractIds.length ? money(r.committed) : '—') },
            { key: 'k', header: 'Commitments', render: (r) => r.contractIds.length ? <span className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">{r.contractIds.map((id) => { const c = s.contracts.find((x) => x.id === id)!; return <button key={id} onClick={() => onOpen(id)} className="text-[12px] font-semibold text-link hover:underline">{c.vendor}</button> })}</span> : r.status === 'company' ? <span className="text-[12px] text-muted">{NON_COMMITMENT_LINES[r.lineId]}</span> : <span className="text-[12px] text-faint">—</span> },
            { key: 's', header: 'Check', render: (r) => <Badge tone={r.status === 'covered' ? 'good' : r.status === 'company' ? 'neutral' : 'warn'}>{r.status === 'covered' ? 'Covered' : r.status === 'company' ? 'Company / direct' : 'Uncovered'}</Badge> },
            { key: 'a', header: '', render: (r) => canEdit && r.status === 'gap' ? <Button size="sm" onClick={() => onCover(r.lineId)}>Add commitment</Button> : null },
          ]} />
        </>
      )}
    </Panel>
  )
}

// ───────────────────────────── Project-wide RFI / CCO log
function CcoLog({ s, p, onOpen }: { s: AppState; p: Project; onOpen: (contractId: string, ccoId: string) => void }) {
  const rows = s.commitmentChanges.filter((x) => x.projectId === p.id).sort((a, b) => b.requestedAt.localeCompare(a.requestedAt) || b.code.localeCompare(a.code))
  if (!rows.length) return null
  return (
    <Panel title="RFI / change order log" subtitle="Every change to what a vendor is paid, with its approval status. Approved amounts are in the commitments above." pad={false}>
      <Table rows={rows} rowKey={(x) => x.id} onRowClick={(x) => onOpen(x.contractId, x.id)} dense columns={[
        { key: 'c', header: 'Code', sort: (x) => x.code, render: (x) => <Mono className="font-semibold">{x.code}</Mono> },
        { key: 'v', header: 'Commitment', render: (x) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap">{s.contracts.find((c) => c.id === x.contractId)?.vendor ?? '—'}</span> },
        { key: 'r', header: 'RFI / field condition', render: (x) => <span className="block min-w-[220px]">{x.rfi}<span className="block text-[11.5px] text-muted">{x.description}</span></span> },
        { key: 'a', header: 'Amount', num: true, sort: (x) => x.amount, render: (x) => <span className={cx('font-semibold', x.amount > 0 ? 'text-bad' : x.amount < 0 ? 'text-good' : '')}>{moneyDelta(x.amount)}</span> },
        { key: 'd', header: 'Days', num: true, render: (x) => (x.scheduleImpactDays ? `+${x.scheduleImpactDays}` : '0') },
        { key: 'ch', header: 'Project change', render: (x) => { const ch = s.changes.find((c) => c.id === x.changeId); return ch ? <a href={projectHref(p.id, 'changes')} onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setFocusChange(ch.id) }} className="font-mono text-[12px] font-semibold text-link hover:underline">{ch.code}</a> : <span className="text-faint">—</span> } },
        { key: 'q', header: 'Requested', sort: (x) => x.requestedAt, render: (x) => <span className="whitespace-nowrap text-[12px]">{fmtDate(x.requestedAt)} · {personName(s, x.requestedById)}</span> },
        { key: 's', header: 'Status', sort: (x) => x.status, render: (x) => <StatusBadge status={x.status} /> },
      ]} />
    </Panel>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/finance/CcoModal.tsx =====
// "New RFI / change order": the single entry point for changing a commitment. Logs the field question and
// its cost against a commitment, optionally ties it to a project change request, and starts the
// 'commitment-change' workflow (PM → construction manager → project controls → approver by dollar authority).
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { mutate, useStore } from '@/core/store'
import { commitmentRow, createCommitmentChange, nextCcoCode } from '@/core/engine/finance'
import { latestDef, simulate } from '@/core/engine/workflow'
import { money, moneyDelta, cx } from '@/core/util'
import { Button, Callout, Field, Input, KV, Modal, MoneyInput, Select, TextArea } from '@/ui/kit'
import { toast } from '@/ui/toast'

export interface CcoSeed { contractId?: string; changeId?: string }

export function CcoModal({ projectId, seed, onClose, onCreated }: { projectId: string; seed: CcoSeed | null; onClose: () => void; onCreated?: (ccoId: string, contractId: string) => void }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === projectId)!
  const contracts = s.contracts.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId && c.status !== 'rfp' && c.status !== 'closed')
  const changes = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === projectId && c.status !== 'rejected')
  const blank = { contractId: '', rfi: '', description: '', amount: 0, days: 0, changeId: '' }
  const [f, setF] = useState(blank)
  const open = !!seed
  useEffect(() => {
    if (seed) setF({ ...blank, contractId: seed.contractId ?? contracts[0]?.id ?? '', changeId: seed.changeId ?? '' })
  }, [open, seed?.contractId, seed?.changeId]) // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const k = s.contracts.find((c) => c.id === f.contractId)
  const row = k ? commitmentRow(s, k) : undefined
  const def = latestDef(s, 'commitment-change')
  const route = useMemo(() => (def && open ? simulate(s, def, { amount: f.amount, scheduleImpactDays: f.days, rfi: f.rfi }, projectId) : []), [s, def, f.amount, f.days, f.rfi, projectId, open])
  if (!seed) return null
  const submit = () => {
    if (!f.contractId) { toast('Pick the commitment this change applies to', 'bad'); return }
    if (!f.rfi.trim() || !f.description.trim()) { toast('Describe the RFI or field condition and the change', 'bad'); return }
    if (!f.amount && !f.days) { toast('Enter the cost or schedule impact', 'bad'); return }
    let id = '', code = ''
    mutate((d) => { const cc = createCommitmentChange(d, projectId, { contractId: f.contractId, rfi: f.rfi, description: f.description, amount: f.amount, scheduleImpactDays: f.days, changeId: f.changeId || undefined }); id = cc.id; code = cc.code })
    toast(`${code} submitted for approval`)
    onCreated?.(id, f.contractId)
    onClose()
  }
  const linked = changes.find((c) => c.id === f.changeId)
  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} wide title={`New RFI / change order · ${nextCcoCode(s, p)}`}
      footer={<><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button><Button variant="primary" onClick={submit} disabled={!contracts.length}>Submit for approval</Button></>}>
      {!contracts.length ? (
        <Callout tone="warn" title="No live commitment">RFI / change orders are raised against an awarded or active commitment. Add the commitment on the Finance tab first.</Callout>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[3fr_2fr]">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <Field label="Commitment">
              <Select id="cco-contract" value={f.contractId} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, contractId: e.target.value })}
                options={contracts.map((c) => ({ value: c.id, label: `${c.vendor} · ${c.type} · PO ${c.poNumber ?? '—'} · ${money(c.value + c.changeOrders)}` }))} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="RFI / field condition" hint="The question or condition that triggered the change">
              <Input id="cco-rfi" value={f.rfi} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, rfi: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Unmarked 12-inch water main at Sta 42+10 conflicts with alignment" />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Change to the commitment">
              <TextArea id="cco-desc" value={f.description} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, description: e.target.value })} placeholder="What the vendor will do differently, how it was priced (unit rates, T&M, lump sum) and the evidence attached." />
            </Field>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Field label="Amount" hint="Negative for a credit"><MoneyInput id="cco-amount" value={f.amount} onChange={(v) => setF({ ...f, amount: v })} /></Field>
              <Field label="Schedule impact (days)"><Input id="cco-days" type="number" value={f.days} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, days: Number(e.target.value) || 0 })} /></Field>
            </div>
            <Field label="Project change request (optional)" hint="Link when the RFI is part of a change to the project baseline">
              <Select id="cco-change" value={f.changeId} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, changeId: e.target.value })}
                options={[{ value: '', label: 'Not linked — absorbed within the commitment' }, ...changes.map((c) => ({ value: c.id, label: `${c.code} ${c.title} (${c.status})` }))]} />
            </Field>
            {linked && linked.contractIds && !linked.contractIds.includes(f.contractId) && <Callout tone="info">{linked.code} will list this commitment as affected once the CCO is submitted.</Callout>}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-md border border-line bg-sunken/40 p-3">
            <div className="eyebrow">Commitment after approval</div>
            {row && <KV cols={1} items={[
              ['Current commitment', <span key="c" className="tabular-nums">{money(row.current, 'full')}</span>],
              ['This change', <b key="t" className={cx('tabular-nums', f.amount > 0 ? 'text-bad' : f.amount < 0 ? 'text-good' : '')}>{moneyDelta(f.amount)}</b>],
              ['In review already', <span key="r" className="tabular-nums">{money(row.pendingChanges)}</span>],
              ['Commitment if approved', <b key="a" className="tabular-nums">{money(row.current + f.amount, 'full')}</b>],
              ['Remaining to invoice', <span key="rm" className="tabular-nums">{money(row.remaining + f.amount, 'full')}</span>],
              ...(row.c.poTotal ? [['Share of PO', <span key="po" className={cx('tabular-nums', row.poShare !== undefined && row.current + f.amount > row.poShare ? 'text-warn font-semibold' : '')}>{(((row.current + f.amount) / row.c.poTotal) * 100).toFixed(1)}% used · {row.c.pctOfPo ?? '—'}% planned</span>] as [string, JSX.Element]] : []),
            ]} />}
            <div className="eyebrow mt-1">Routing (live from the workflow)</div>
            <ol className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-[12.5px]">
              {route.map((r, i) => <li key={i} className="flex gap-2"><span className="w-4 text-right tabular-nums text-faint">{i + 1}</span><span className="min-w-0"><b>{r.node.label}</b><span className="block text-muted">{r.people.map((x) => x.name).join(', ') || 'Unassigned'}</span></span></li>)}
            </ol>
            <p className="text-[11.5px] text-muted">Approval adds the amount to the commitment's approved changes. Nobody edits a commitment value directly.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Modal>
  )
}

===== FILE: src/views/project/tabs/finance/CommitmentDetail.tsx =====
// One commitment: PO share, the money waterfall (committed → approved changes → current → invoiced → remaining),
// data-quality flags, and its RFI / change order log with the live workflow for each.
import { useState } from 'react'
import { personName, useStore } from '@/core/store'
import type { CommitmentChange } from '@/core/types'
import { commitmentIssues, commitmentRow } from '@/core/engine/finance'
import { lineById } from '@/core/engine/lines'
import { projectHref } from '@/core/router'
import { fmtDate, money, moneyDelta, cx } from '@/core/util'
import { Badge, Button, Callout, Drawer, Empty, KV, Mono, Progress, Section, StatusBadge } from '@/ui/kit'
import { Icon } from '@/ui/icons'
import { WorkflowStatus } from '../../../shared/WorkflowStatus'
import { setFocusChange } from '../controls/trace'

export function CommitmentDetail({ contractId, focusCco, onClose, onEdit, onNewCco, canEdit = true }: { contractId: string; focusCco?: string; onClose: () => void; onEdit: () => void; onNewCco: () => void; canEdit?: boolean }) {
  const s = useStore()
  const c = s.contracts.find((x) => x.id === contractId)
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string | null>(focusCco ?? null)
  if (!c) return null
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === c.projectId)!
  const r = commitmentRow(s, c)
  const issues = commitmentIssues(s, p, c)
  const io = p.finance?.ios.find((x) => x.id === c.ioId)
  const billed = r.current ? r.invoiced / r.current : 0
  const live = c.status !== 'rfp' && c.status !== 'closed'
  return (
    <Drawer open onClose={onClose} width={680} title={<span className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{c.vendor}<Badge>{c.type}</Badge><StatusBadge status={c.status} label={c.status === 'rfp' ? 'RFP' : undefined} /></span>}
      footer={canEdit ? <><Button variant="ghost" onClick={onEdit}>Edit commitment</Button>{live && <Button variant="primary" icon={<Icon name="plus" className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />} onClick={onNewCco}>New RFI / change order</Button>}</> : undefined}>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <KV cols={3} items={[
